On two Cell Tracking Challenge sequences, a C. elegans embryo and a Chinese Hamster Ovarian (CHO), with fidelity scored per cell nucleus, our reconstruction holds the highest nucleus fidelity at every distance from an acquired frame, has the flattest decay across the gap, and best recovers focal planes it was never shown with graceful degradation across the gap.

</details>


### [37] [Sparse-GS2Mesh: 3D Gaussian Splatting Guided by Novel Stereo Views and 2DGS for Sparse View Surface Reconstruction}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04203)
*Younghyun Noh,Minje Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sparse-GS2Mesh 提出了一种针对稀疏视角条件的立体感知表面重建框架，通过使用共轭深度先验初始化 3DGS、自适应基线选择、立体匹配网络细调以及 2D/3D 共正则化细调，来改善表面重建效果，从而在无明显重叠和部分重叠设置中分别提高了 15% 和保持了与最先进的方法相当的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表面重建方法在稀疏视角和无明显重叠条件下效果不佳，为此 Sparse-GS2Mesh 提出了一种新颖的框架来解决这个问题。

Method: Sparse-GS2Mesh 利用共轭深度先验初始化 3DGS，并结合自适应基线选择、立体匹配网络细调以及 2D/3D 共正则化细调来实现稀疏视角条件下的表面重建。

Result: Sparse-GS2Mesh 在无明显重叠设置上实现了 15% 的性能提升，在部分重叠设置中取得了与最先进的方法相当的结果。

Conclusion: 该框架可在稀疏视角设置中产生更准确和稳定的表面重建结果。

Abstract: Surface reconstruction under sparse-view settings remains challenging due to limited geometric cues. Volume rendering methods based on signed distance functions often produce over-smoothed surfaces, while 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), though time-efficient, suffers from incomplete geometry due to the lack of reliable depth supervision and the limitation of being optimized only from given input views. In this paper, we present Sparse-GS2Mesh, a stereo-aware framework for surface reconstruction from sparse views. While 3DGS and stereo matching have been leveraged for surface reconstruction under dense view settings, we extend them to operate effectively under sparse view conditions by first initializing 3DGS using epipolar depth priors to mitigate the 3DGS overfitting problem, followed by our three key components: (I) adaptive baseline selection, (II) fine-tuning with a stereo matching network, and (III) 2D/3D co-regularized fine-tuning. Given a warmed-up 3DGS initialized with epipolar depth, the adaptive baseline selection automatically determines a baseline to synthesize for each sparse view. We then fine-tune 3DGS by backpropagating depth-refining gradients from the stereo matching network, effectively specializing the 3DGS for stereo matching. The 2D/3D co-regularization further helps obtain stable reconstruction, addressing weak geometric cues in close stereo views. Sparse-GS2Mesh achieves a 15\% improvement over state-of-the-art methods in little-overlap settings and comparable results in large-overlap settings. Codes will be publicly available.

</details>


### [38] [FlashGaze: Training-Free Multi-Scale Patch Pruning For Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04225)
*Ziye Zhu,Yanghao Zhou,Lixing Tan,Jialiang Kang,Shuxuan Li,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashGaze 是一种无需训练的方法，在 ViT 编码前减少时空冗余，无需引入辅助网络。它使用像素空间差异作为信息丢失的代理，并使用 Quadtree 动态规划来在一个固定预算下同时优化补丁删除、合并和保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法在处理长、高分辨率视频时存在效率问题。FlashGaze 提出了一种解决方案，旨在提高处理此类视频的效率，同时维持相当的准确度。

Method: FlashGaze 使用像素空间差异作为信息丢失的代理，并利用 Quadtree 动态规划来在一个固定预算下同时优化补丁删除、合并和保持。

Result: 在两个 MLLM 主干网络上进行的不同基准测试表明，FlashGaze 能显著提高效率，同时保有较高的准确度。在实验中，FlashGaze 在 Qwen3-VL-8B 上保留了 98% 的全输入基线准确度，同时实现了高达 5.4 倍和 17 倍的视频编码和预填充速度提升，并减少了高达 1.8 倍的峰值 GPU 内存使用量。

Conclusion: FlashGaze 在保持准确性的前提下显著提高了处理长、高分辨率视频的效率，有望在未来大规模视频理解任务中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in video understanding, yet efficiently processing long, high-resolution videos remains challenging. Such videos often contain substantial spatiotemporal redundancy, and processing redundant visual tokens can incur avoidable computational overhead. Many existing methods prune visual tokens during or after vision transformer (ViT) encoding, leaving much of the encoding cost unaddressed. Some approaches prune patches before encoding but rely on learned auxiliary networks for patch selection, incurring additional training and inference overhead. To address these limitations, we propose FlashGaze, a training-free method that reduces spatiotemporal redundancy before ViT encoding without introducing auxiliary networks. FlashGaze uses pixel-space differences as a proxy for information loss and employs Quadtree Dynamic Programming to jointly optimize patch dropping, merging, and keeping under a fixed budget. Experiments on two MLLM backbones across multiple benchmarks demonstrate substantial efficiency gains while largely preserving accuracy. On Qwen3-VL-8B, FlashGaze retains 98% of the full-input baseline accuracy on LongVideoBench while achieving up to 5.4x and 17x speedups in ViT encoding and MLLM prefill, respectively, and reducing peak GPU memory usage by a factor of 1.8. These efficiency gains enable the model to process videos with more frames and higher resolutions on the same GPU hardware, unlocking video understanding at scales previously out of reach.

</details>


### [39] [OctMesh: A Unified Octree-Hierarchical Framework for Lossless Triangle Mesh Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04281)
*Shiyu Feng,Xihua Sheng,Lingyu Zhu,Chunyang Fu,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctMesh 提出了一种基于八叉树层级结构的学习框架，用于同时编码三角网格的几何和连通性。该方法通过将八叉树聚池化后的节点划分为不同类型并预测连接方式，实现高效无损压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的八叉树编码不适用于三角网格，主要因为其连通性难于建模，OctMesh 通过结合八叉树特征和神经预测来克服这一点。

Method: OctMesh 方法首先利用八叉树聚池化定义父母节点的连接类型，然后根据父母节点的不同将孩子节点分组，每个组包含从单一或两个连接的父母节点派生的孩子。该方法定义了四个小任务类别，通过神经预测器估计剩余候选的方法占比，以引导二进制算术编码。同时，提出了一种图形感知父特征提取器，用于整合局部几何信息、父母连通性和全局形状。

Result: 在 MPEG V-DMC 测试序列 256 帧的数据上，OctMesh 以平均每面 7.033 比特实现无损压缩，比 V-Mesh 低 12.8%。此外，该层级表示还支持三角网格的九级渐进式细化。

Conclusion: OctMesh 成功解决了八叉树编码在三角网格应用中的问题，并展示了其在无损压缩和逐级细化方面的优越性能。

Abstract: Lossless triangle mesh compression must preserve both vertex positions and connectivity. Octrees support learned point cloud geometry coding and progressive refinement, but extending them to meshes requires a compatible connectivity representation. Unlike the eight occupancy decisions of a voxel, a parent edge can develop into varied child connections, making edge refinement difficult to model with a compact prediction prior. We propose OctMesh, a learned framework that codes geometry and connectivity on a shared octree hierarchy. Its key observation is that octree pooling produces parents with either one child or two to eight children. Child edges are then grouped by their endpoint parents' types and whether the endpoints share a parent. Each candidate group contains children from just one parent or two connected parents. The resulting four categories define small, fixed-shape prediction tasks: connections uniquely determined by the parent graph are inherited without bits, while three neural predictors estimate probabilities for the remaining candidates. These probabilities guide arithmetic coding of the actual edge symbols. Binarized predictions of within-parent connections provide context for predicting connections between different parents. A graph-aware parent feature extractor combines local geometry, parent connectivity and global shape. The connectivity models use dedicated weights at coarse levels and share weights at fine levels. Residual edges and a finest-level face-selection payload complete the reconstruction. On 256 frames from eight MPEG V-DMC test sequences, OctMesh losslessly recovers the finest-level vertex-coordinate, edge and unoriented face sets at an average of 7.033 bits per face, 12.8% below V-Mesh. The same hierarchical representation supports nine levels of progressive vertex-and-edge refinement.

</details>


### [40] [A Geometric-Transformation Feature-Adaptive Manifold Restoration Method for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation of Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04300)
*Jianzheng Wang,Huan Ni,Xiaonan Niu,Danfeng Hong,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个基于D4群几何变换的特征自适应流形修复方法，旨在提高SAM3在遥感图像中的开放词汇语义分割稳定性，试验结果显示该方法在多个基准上的平均mIoU为55.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于SAM3的开放词汇语义分割方法在处理不同几何变换时表现不一致，该研究旨在通过利用几何不变性来提高其稳定性。

Method: 首先引入了多尺度谐波导向的D4视角选择(MH-D4VS)，然后提出了原视图锚定的自适应流形修复(OAMR)，最后开发了针对SAM3的像素解码器测试时自适应调整(PD-TTA)。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法在八个遥感语义分割基准上的平均mIoU为55.6%，并且在不同SAM3推理框架下表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提出的方法能够有效提升SAM3在遥感图像中的开放词汇语义分割稳定性，并在多个基准上取得了显著的性能改进。

Abstract: The semantic information of objects in remote sensing images is typically invariant to geometric transformations from the dihedral group D4. However, SAM3-based open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) methods often exhibit inconsistent responses to different geometric transformations. To exploit this property and improve the stability of OVSS for remote sensing images, we propose a feature-adaptive manifold repair method based on dihedral-group geometric transformations. First, we introduce multi-scale harmonic-guided D4 view selection (MH-D4VS) to select complementary candidate views from a set of geometrically transformed views. Next, we propose original-view-anchored adaptive manifold repair (OAMR), which uses the original view as an anchor and reliable cross-view information to selectively repair locally unreliable visual features. Finally, we develop pixel decoder test-time adaptation (PD-TTA) for SAM3, which fine-tunes only the parameters of the GroupNorm layers online during inference, thereby enhancing the model's ability to adapt to sample-level distribution shifts. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves an average mIoU of 55.6% across eight remote sensing semantic segmentation benchmarks and delivers consistent performance improvements under different SAM3-based inference frameworks.

</details>


### [41] [Rethinking Long-Video Efficiency: A Joint Allocation Perspective on Frames, Pixels, and Front-End Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04318)
*Sixun Dong,Wei Li,Andong Deng,Qi Qian,Victor Zhu,Zhengping Ji,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoHi，一种训练免费的一次通过框架，通过VLM的视频和图像路径结合了密集的低分辨率视频流和稀疏的高分辨率图像帧，显著提高了长视频理解的准确性和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长视频理解方法通常聚焦于在固定分辨率下选择信息丰富的帧。研究发现，低分辨率的密集采样在匹配的标记预算下优于高分辨率的稀疏采样；对分辨率敏感的任务可以从选定的高分辨率帧中受益；前端解码在观看一小时的视频中占据了大部分时间。这些发现促使提出了LoHi框架。

Method: LoHi框架结合了低分辨率的密集视频流和高分辨率的部分图像帧。通过VLM的视觉路径和语言路径分别处理这两种类型的帧，通过特定的策略选择高分辨率的帧，以提高模型在长视频理解中的性能。

Result: 在三个长视频基准数据集上，LoHi在匹配标记预算的情况下提高了10.6个百分点的平均准确率，比最强的先前方法提高了5.2个百分点，并将一小时视频的前端解码延迟减少了7倍。

Conclusion: LoHi框架通过灵活地利用低分辨率帧和高分辨率帧，大幅度提高了长期视频理解的效率和准确性，为该领域的研究提供了新的启示。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding with vision-language models (VLMs) is often framed as selecting informative frames or visual tokens at a fixed native resolution. We show that per-frame resolution can instead be traded for denser temporal coverage, while front-end decoding latency depends on the size of the candidate pool rather than the final token budget. An empirical study across multiple VLMs and long-video benchmarks yields three findings: dense low-resolution sampling outperforms sparse native-resolution sampling at matched token budgets; resolution-sensitive tasks benefit from selected high-resolution frames; and front-end decoding dominates wall time for hour-long videos. Motivated by these findings, we introduce LoHi, a training-free, single-pass framework that combines a dense low-resolution video stream with sparse high-resolution image frames through the VLM's native video and image pathways. LoHi-Anchor selects high-resolution frames using codec I-frame metadata, while LoHi-SemDiv uses query relevance and visual diversity over CLIP features. Across three long-video benchmarks, LoHi improves average accuracy by 10.6 percentage points over the native-resolution baseline at a matched token budget and by 5.2 percentage points over the strongest prior efficiency method. It also reduces front-end decoding latency by up to 7x on hour-long videos. Project page: https://sixundong.com/projects/lohi

</details>


### [42] [Trust the View That Sees the Target: Mining Cross-View Conflicts for Reliability-Gated Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04327)
*Yifan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究专注于两种独立训练的单视角模型在评估建筑物损坏时产生分歧的情况。通过分析森林野火和飓风数据集，发现如果直接信任正确的视角而非融合视角，准确度提升显著。研究提出了一种基于视图可见性条件的可靠度门控方法来决定应信任哪一视角，并且这种方法在某些情况下的表现超过了端到端融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多数方法在评估建筑物损坏时会信任从上方和地面采集的图像一样多的信息，但不完全准确。该研究旨在找到特定情况下应更依赖哪一种视角，通过对未被正确融合的情况（即冲突案例）进行分析，提出了一种新的方法来选择更可靠的视角。

Method: 首先通过三个配对数据集（森林野火和飓风）中的冲突案例，这些案例占了相当大的比例。然后识别出其中一个视角比另一个更可靠，并提出一种基于视图可见性条件的可靠度门控方法来决定应信任哪个视角。最后，进行了一个在视野方面受控的实验来进一步解释该方法的效果。

Result: 通过一种可校准的基于可见性的可靠度门控方法，可比融合方法获得更好的效果。这种新方法对冲突案例的准确度提升了0.051，而对于全景数据集的效果和融合方法相当。此外，研究还发现视野范围对决定应信任哪一视角产生了重要影响。

Conclusion: 该研究强调，单纯的信任融合视角并不总是最优的选择，而该研究提出的方法可以在特定情况下提高建筑物评估的准确性。未来的评估工作可以通过基于这种可见性门控的方法来更好地选择视角，从而提高评估的准确性。

Abstract: After a disaster, building damage is assessed from overhead tiles and ground-level photographs, and most methods fuse the two views symmetrically, trusting both equally for every building. This paper focuses on the samples where that assumption fails: the conflict cases, on which two independently trained single-view models disagree. We mine such cases from three paired collections (inspection photographs from the 2025 Eaton wildfire and street-view panoramas from Hurricanes Ian and Milton, each matched to very-high-resolution overhead tiles), where they make up 10-33% of the data. On these samples an oracle that simply trusts the correct view beats every fusion method we tested by 0.37-0.41 accuracy, and the gap survives longer training, calibration, and backbone changes. We recover part of it with a visibility-conditioned reliability gate: a linear model that decides which view to trust from building-visibility features, calibrated per-view confidences, and the disagreement itself. On the wildfire data the gate is the only method that significantly beats calibrated probability averaging (+0.051 on conflicts, p=0.0001) and end-to-end fusion (+0.072, p<10^-4); on the panoramic datasets it matches them. A controlled field-of-view experiment explains why: cropping panoramas toward the building doubles the benefit of fusion, whereas random crops of the same size do not. Finally, the spatial density of conflicts predicts tile-level damage without labels (Spearman r=0.615, p=0.001). Mining conflicts turns "does fusion help?" into "which view should be trusted, where, and why?".

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Vision Transformer (ViT) 的单目无人机姿态估计模型在合成数据上的训练能力，并通过实测数据验证了其泛化性能，引入了姿态模糊性意识策略和α-β滤波器以提高姿态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于无人机难以预先影响和装备，通过图像估计其姿态对于推进无人机自动导航和避障等任务非常重要。然而，基于深度学习的方法受到大规模真实环境数据集获取上的高成本和难度的限制。合成图像提供了一种替代方法，但模型必须解决合成到真实领域的差距才能适应实际图像。

Method: 本文采用自监督的DINOv2骨干网络，仅使用标记的合成图像进行训练，然后在标记的真实图像上进行评估。引入姿态模糊性意识策略和α-β滤波器改进姿态估计。

Result: 在不含过滤器的情况下，模型在真实数据集上达到19.18°的均方角误差(MAE)和13.25 ms的推理时间，过滤后精度提高至8.74°，推理时间增加至13.42 ms。

Abstract: Remote pose estimation of noncooperative Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) from imagery is critical, as they cannot be influenced or instrumented in advance. Deep-learning-based approaches offer a promising solution; however, their development is constrained by the cost and difficulty of acquiring large-scale real-world datasets with accurate pose labels. Synthetic imagery provides an alternative, but models trained on synthetic data must overcome the synthetic-to-real domain gap to generalize to real-world imagery. This work investigates the inherent synthetic-to-real generalization capability of a Vision Transformer (ViT)-based model for monocular UAV pose estimation. The proposed approach employs a self-supervised DINOv2 backbone and is trained exclusively on labeled synthetic imagery while being evaluated on labeled real-world imagery. Pose ambiguity-aware strategies are incorporated during training and inference to address ambiguities arising from the projection of a three-dimensional target onto a two-dimensional image plane and from target symmetries. An $α$-$β$ filter is further integrated during inference to improve pose estimations. To assess the model under operational requirements, it is evaluated in terms of Mean Angular Error (MAE) and inference time, both before and after filtering, using a real-world dataset containing 77,077 labeled UAV images. Before filtering, the model achieves an MAE of $19.18^{\circ}$ and an inference time of $13.25$ ms, whereas after filtering, these values are $8.74^{\circ}$ and $13.42$ ms, respectively.

</details>

*Tian Xu,Soroush Atashi,Tianju Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不同的拉格朗日耦合3DGS-SPH模型，用于变形固体的前向模拟和反向分析，并通过数值研究验证了模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来生成世界模型的发展引起了对能够再现现实物体外观及其物理作用响应的数字模型的兴趣。现有技术虽然能够捕捉表面几何和外观，但在扩展这些表示到机械可解释的模型方面仍存在不足。

Method: 一种利用可微拉格朗日耦合3DGS-SPH模型进行变形固体的前向模拟和反向分析的方法。首先从多视角校准的视觉数据集中重建观察到的对象，然后通过包络法为固体力学模型生成独立的SPH支持。

Result: 该方法在数值研究中验证了其对有限元结果的SPH响应的准确性，还评估了与常规模型的准确性和效率，并展示了对梁、桥梁和肝脏形状的前向模拟示例。反向分析进一步从渲染变形观测中估算材料和边界参数，证明了从图像数据实现基于力学参数识别的可能性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，所提出的模型在机械参数识别方面的实用性，并表明了从图像数据进行力学建模的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative world models have increased interest in digital models that reproduce both the appearance of real objects and their response to physical interaction. Three-dimensional reconstruction techniques, including 3D Gaussian Splatting, capture detailed surface geometry and appearance from images and videos. However, extending these representations beyond plausible animation to mechanically interpretable models for constitutive behavior, boundary conditions, and inverse parameter identification remains less explored. In this work, a differentiable Lagrangian-coupled 3DGS-smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH) model is proposed for forward simulation and inverse analysis of deformable solids. The observed object is first reconstructed from multi-view calibrated visual dataset as a 3DGS rendering model. An envelope-based procedure then generates an independent SPH support for the solid-mechanics model, avoiding the direct use of rendering primitives as mechanical particles. A reference-configuration Lagrangian transfer maps SPH deformation to Gaussian positions and covariances, thereby coupling the physical model and the image observation model while preserving a differentiable computational path. The SPH formulation supports linear elastic, hyperelastic, and Kelvin--Voigt viscoelastic responses, together with fixed, free, and Robin-type boundary conditions. Numerical studies validate the SPH response against finite-element results, assess accuracy and efficiency against a conventional model using Gaussian centers as surface SPH particles, and demonstrate forward simulations on beam, bridge, and liver-shaped examples. Inverse analyses further estimate constitutive and boundary parameters from rendered deformation observations, including noisy cases, demonstrating the feasibility of the proposed model for mechanics-based parameter identification from image data.

</details>

*Kazutoshi Akita,Norimichi Ukita*

Method: 本文提出了一种基于预测尺度建议图（Scale Proposal Maps）的方法，该方法能够预测每个超分辨率尺度上合适的像素集合，从而抑制假阳性检测。

Result: 通过使用本文的方法，可以在连续调整对象图像尺度的同时实现稳定且准确的对象检测，避免了直接将多个超分辨率图像独立输入对象检测器所产生过多的假阳性检测。

Conclusion: 本文的方法证明了在对象检测中使用连续尺度的优势，并提出了一种全新的方法来解决假阳性问题。

Abstract: This paper proposes any-scale object detection using arbitrary-scale super-resolution for continuously rescaling object images, while general multi-scale object detection uses discretely rescaled appearance representations. However, a naive usage of super-resolution produces many false-positive detections if many super-resolution images are independently fed into an object detector. Our method suppresses these false positives by predicting scale proposal maps, each of which represents a set of pixels appropriate for each super-resolution scale.

</details>


### [46] [LoCoSplat: Real-Time Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Minimal 3D Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04351)
*Sinan Wang,Jinjin He,Yuchen Sun,Duowen Chen,Shenyifan Lu,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoCoSplat通过局部上下文加权平均预测点云的尺度、旋转和透明度，无需深度学习的3D网络且计算轻量，实现了比现有方法更快更精确的6视图、12视图和24视图的场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为高斯分布是一个局部的基本单元，深度预测后，3D阶段需要添加的尺度、旋转和透明度取决于每个锚点周围的点云区域，因此利用固定邻域的平均值就能满足需求，无需复杂的3D网络。

Method: LoCoSplat首先将点特征转换为16维线性投影，然后将它们分别投射到细网格和粗网格上。在每个锚点处，通过一个具有140K参数的点乘MLP来读取这两个网格的信息。整个编码器以fp16格式在CUDA图中运行。

Result: 在RealEstate10K数据集上，LoCoSplat在6视图、12视图和24视图下的PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS值均优于所有先前的直接法。在24视图下，与VolSplat相比，PSNR提高了3.3个点。这种优势随着视图密度的增加而进一步扩大，并且在零样本转移到ACID以及在ScanNet上的微调后，性能进一步提升。对于6视图场景的重建，LoCoSplat仅需33毫秒，比先前最先进的方法快4.2倍，训练速度提高了2.7倍（在24视图下提升5.9倍），且在推断时所需的内存仅为之前最先进方法的六分之一。

Conclusion: LoCoSplat方法提供了一种高效且精确的直接法，特别适用于场景重建任务，尤其在高视图密度和大规模点云数据处理方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) increasingly aggregates multi-view evidence with heavy learned 3D networks. We propose LoCoSplat (Local-Context Splatting), motivated by the observation that a Gaussian is a local primitive: once depth is predicted, what the 3D stage must add (scale, rotation, opacity) depends on the point cloud around each anchor, and a fixed local average of that neighbourhood is enough to supply it, no heavy network required. LoCoSplat realises exactly this average: it splats a 16-d linear projection of the point features into a fine and a coarse grid and reads both back at each anchor with a 0.14M-parameter pointwise MLP; with no learned 3D network and no dynamic sparse computation, its whole encoder runs as one fp16 CUDA graph. On RealEstate10K, LoCoSplat outperforms every prior feed-forward method on PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS at 6, 12, and 24 views, with a margin that widens as views densify (+3.3 PSNR over VolSplat, the prior voxel-aligned state of the art, at 24 views) and grows further under zero-shot transfer to ACID and fine-tuning on ScanNet. It reconstructs a 6-view scene in 33 ms on one NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU, the fastest of seven feed-forward methods and $4.2\times$ faster than the previous state of the art, trains $2.7\times$ faster ($5.9\times$ at 24 views), and uses $6.7\times$ less inference memory.

</details>


### [47] [SelectOccFlow: Selective Spatiotemporal Aggregation for 3D Occupancy and Scene Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04356)
*Yuhang Wang,Kai Luo,Yuanfan Zheng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SelectOccFlow 提出了一个选择性的时空聚合框架，通过逐步细化跨图像、时间和体素域的上下文证据，解决了相机占用和场景流预测中的时空聚合不可靠性问题，特别是在语义不兼容、历史观察错位和体素结构不完整等方面。该框架通过语义引导采样、状态条件化时间聚合和方向感知空间聚合改进了体素表示的结构完整性。实验表明，相较于之前的最佳方法，SelectOccFlow 在 OpenOcc 上提高了 4.2% 的 OccScore，并在 nuScenes-C 版本的均值 OccScore 上提高了 11.1%，展示了更强的视觉干扰鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机的占用和场景流预测方法对时空聚合的不可靠性敏感，受语义不兼容的图像特征、未对齐的历史观测和不完整的体素结构影响。SelectOccFlow 框架旨在通过逐步细化跨图像、时间和体素域的上下文证据，提高时空聚合的可靠性。

Method: SelectOccFlow 引入了语义引导采样（SGS）、状态条件化时间聚合（SCTA）和方向感知空间聚合（ESA）等技术。SGS 调整了基于语义先验的特征采样，SCTA 根据体素状态选择性地检索历史证据，ESA 利用方向性结构支持来改进前景几何的结构完整性。

Result: 实验结果表明，SelectOccFlow 在 OpenOcc 上获得了 44.9 的最佳 OccScore，比之前的方法提高了 4.2%，并且在 Occ3D-nus 和 nuScenes-C 上的性能也有所改进，特别是在视觉干扰下具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SelectOccFlow 提供了一个强大的框架，增强了基于相机的占用和场景流预测性能，特别是在处理视觉干扰方面展现了其优势。未来的研究可以进一步优化该方法，以适应更复杂的驾驶场景。

Abstract: Comprehensive 3D scene understanding for autonomous driving requires modeling geometry, semantics, and motion. However, camera-based occupancy and scene flow prediction are sensitive to unreliable spatial and temporal aggregation, caused by semantically incompatible image features, misaligned historical observations, and incomplete voxel structures. To address this issue, we propose SelectOccFlow, a selective spatiotemporal aggregation framework that progressively refines contextual evidence across image, temporal, and voxel domains. To obtain semantically compatible image evidence, we design Semantic-Guided Sampling (SGS) to regulate feature sampling with semantic priors. Since reliable image evidence alone cannot resolve temporal inconsistency, we then present State-Conditioned Temporal Aggregation (SCTA) to selectively retrieve historical evidence according to voxel states. To further enhance the structural completeness of voxel representations, we introduce Extent-Aware Spatial Aggregation (ESA), which exploits directional structural support to refine foreground geometry. Experiments on OpenOcc demonstrate that SelectOccFlow achieves a state-of-the-art OccScore of 44.9, improving the previous best by +4.2%. It also maintains competitive occupancy performance on Occ3D-nus and improves the mean OccScore under nuScenes-C corruptions by +11.1%, demonstrating improved robustness to visual corruptions. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/muchen1021/SelectOccFlow.

</details>


### [48] [TRIM-ReID: Duplication-Aware Token Reduction and Modality-Aligned Interaction for Multi-Modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04361)
*Wanke Xia,Ruiding Zhu,Xingguo Xu,Zhengbo Zhang,Dongxia Liu,Yuan Jin,Taojie Zhu,Yiting Zhao,Yihang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRIM-ReID 提出了一种紧凑框架，通过密集特征提取、模态内标记减少和跨模态对齐交互来提高多模态目标再识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态目标再识别方法依赖于全局图像-文本对齐的视觉编码器，可能会丢失细粒度的身份线索，并且无法显式地处理标记冗余，导致局部证据不足和标记重复，这会造成跨模态交互的噪声和成本增加。

Method: TRIM-ReID 使用密集身份表示（DIR）提取语义丰富且空间上一致的块特征，利用 DINOv3 保留细微的身份信息。引入标记多样性挖掘（TDM）以识别互补的本地证据，并通过抑制重复块来构建紧凑的模态特定标记集，以保持信息的多样性。保留的标记由模态关系交互（MRI）融合以实现跨模态的有效信息交换，triangular 对齐损失则用来显式地规范化跨模态的联合关系，确保独立选标记下的跨模态语义一致性。

Result: TRIM-ReID 在 RGBNT201、RGBNT100 和 MSVR310 数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: TRIM-ReID 通过紧凑的框架改进了多模态目标再识别技术，有效地处理了模态内标记减少和跨模态对齐交互的问题，取得了显著的性能提升，对于多模态场景理解具有重要价值。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification exploits complementary RGB, near-infrared (NIR), and thermal-infrared (TIR) observations to retrieve target objects. However, existing methods commonly employ visual encoders optimized for global image-text alignment and select tokens using learned importance scores. Such designs fail to preserve fine-grained identity cues or explicitly account for token redundancy, resulting in underrepresented local evidence and duplicated tokens that lead to noisy and costly cross-modal interaction. To address this gap, we propose TRIM-ReID, a compact framework that unifies dense feature extraction, intra-modal token reduction, and inter-modal aligned interaction. Specifically, semantically rich and spatially coherent patch features are extracted by Dense Identity Representation (DIR), which leverages DINOv3 to preserve fine-grained identity information. We then introduce Token Diversity Mining (TDM) to identify complementary local evidence and construct compact modality-specific token sets by suppressing repetitive patches while preserving informative diversity. Retained tokens are subsequently fused by Modal Relational Interaction (MRI) to enable effective information exchange across modalities, while a triangular alignment loss explicitly regularizes their joint relationships to maintain cross-modal semantic consistency under independent token selection. Extensive experiments on RGBNT201, RGBNT100, and MSVR310 demonstrate that TRIM-ReID achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Plausibility: Verifiable Fine-Grained Image Editing on Structured Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04381)
*Muyao Wang,Chen Zhu,Shiqi Yang,DongHyun Hwan,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeriEdit-Bench 提供了一种基准，用于评估图像编辑模型在现实结构资产上的细粒度指令忠实度编辑能力，通过确定性的四轴评估，使评估结果能够得到验证，推动模型能力的透明度和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准在真实性和可验证性之间存在差距，在现实结构资产上的精细指令忠实度编辑难以精确评估。

Method: VeriEdit-Bench 使用来自153个SVG图形、图表、网页接口和演示文稿的源代码，进行受控源代码编辑，从而生成精确的目标图像、像素级的编辑掩码和明确的编辑规范，通过四轴评估实现了可重复的评分。

Result: 评估了11个编辑器，发现即使是最强劲的模型也难以获得满分；在不同类型的资产中，相同的颜色重新着色操作的排名会反转；具有相似像素准确性分布的输出在定位和变化幅度上仍可能有显著差异。四轴分解提供了分级、可验证的反馈，凸显了模型的特定能力及其失败模式。

Conclusion: VeriEdit-Bench 通过四轴评估改进了图像编辑的可验证性，为模型的精确编辑能力提供了一种透明的方法，有助于推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Fine-grained image editing requires more than producing a visually plausible result: an editor must execute the requested attribute change precisely while leaving everything else intact. However, existing benchmarks leave a critical gap between realism and verifiability: benchmarks built on realistic images typically rely on human or vision--language model judgments, while deterministic evaluation has largely focused on synthetic shape canvases, with application-oriented extensions primarily limited to charts. This makes it difficult to determine precisely how much of a requested edit was executed, where unintended changes occurred, and whether small differences between models reflect genuine editing capability or evaluator uncertainty. To bridge this gap, we present VeriEdit-Bench, a benchmark for fine-grained, instruction-faithful image editing across realistic structured assets with deterministic, four-axis evaluation. Its 1,740 cases are compiled from the source code of 153 Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) graphics, charts, web interfaces, and presentation slides. Controlled source-code edits preserve the original visual context while yielding exact target images, pixel-level edit masks, and explicit edit specifications, enabling reproducible scoring along four axes: edit fidelity, preservation, localization, and magnitude. Evaluating eleven editors, we find that even the strongest model remains far from full credit; rankings for the same recoloring operation reverse between charts and SVG graphics; and outputs with similar pixel-accuracy profiles can still differ substantially in localization and change magnitude. This decomposition yields graded, verifiable feedback and exposes model-specific capability and failure profiles that holistic scores or evaluator-dependent judgments may obscure.

</details>


### [50] [Detecting Defects that Matter: An Application-Driven Benchmark for Anomaly Detection in Manufacturing and Retail Logistics (VAND 4.0 Challenge)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04392)
*Lars Heckler-Kram,Dorian Henning,Ashwin Vaidya,Jan-Hendrik Neudeck,Ulla Scheler,Anton Milan,Samet Akcay,Paula Ramos,Sebastian Höfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究引入了VAND 4.0挑战，旨在评估工业制造和零售物流领域的异常检测方法。实证研究揭示了当前方法在处理稀有缺陷及分布变化时的局限性，并提出需关注模型效率及开发适用于稀有缺陷和变化条件的真正异常检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异常检测基准通常过于饱和且缺乏实际应用性，因此该研究旨在提出新的评价基准，以推动相关领域的技术发展。

Method: 研究通过两个关键领域的应用驱动基准测试来评估现有方法的效果，包括基于工业制造和零售物流的测试赛道。在每个赛道中，研究人员详细分析了不同方法的表现。

Result: 研究结果表明，在工业制造赛道中，现有方法在处理罕见缺陷时表现不佳，而零售物流赛道则揭示了当前模型无法完全替代专用于特定任务的模型。此外，研究还发现在处理稀有缺陷和分布变化时，模型性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，未来需要关注模型的计算效率，开发更适用于罕见缺陷和变化条件的真正异常检测方法。

Abstract: Existing Anomaly Detection benchmarks are saturated and often unrealistic. As part of the VAND 4.0 Challenge, we introduce a hidden-test, application-driven benchmark across two deployment-critical domains: industrial manufacturing and retail logistics. In the Industrial Track (MVTec AD 2), the results reveal that unsupervised anomaly segmentation remains challenging: the best regular-setting method achieves only ~57\% pixel-level $SegF_1$, indicating substantial room for improvement. Zero-shot approaches trail by ~15 $SegF_1$ points, confirming that task-specific training on normal data remains essential for precise defect localization. Robustness to distribution shifts remains a key open challenge and DINOv3-backbones clearly dominate this track. In the Retail Track (Kaputt 2), the results reveal that (1) supervised defect detection is approaching saturation for common defect types; (2) the best off-the-shelf VLM approach trails specialized models by ~28 AP, confirming that currently VLMs cannot replace fine-tuned detectors, (3) reference images did not prove helpful for top-performing approaches. Performance collapses on rare defects (spillage ~53 AP, missing units ~27 AP), where the supervised ceiling is bounded by data availability. To drive future progress in this domain, we provide a new low-prevalence retail AD dataset (Kaputt-Rare). Across both tracks, computational efficiency is assessed as a first-class metric combining performance, throughput, memory, and power consumption. We introduce a novel metric for measuring efficiency and reveal that that top-performing methods rely on heavy architectures while efficiency is largely neglected. Overall, we conclude that the community needs (a) more efficiency-aware method development, and (b) true anomaly detection approaches for rare defects and shifting conditions. https://sites.google.com/view/vand4-cvpr2026/challenge

</details>


### [51] [AgroGround: Multi-Granularity Grounded Recognition in Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04425)
*Abdulla Alshehhi,Zongyan Han,Rao Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究介绍了AgroGround数据集，这是一个用于识别植物疾病和其他农业目标并定位其图像区域的大规模数据集。研究采用了自动化管道从多个农业VQA数据集中提取标注，生成794,850个指令示例，并通过结合目标定位和识别的指令来精调共享的视觉-语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的农业视觉数据集缺乏对图像区域的标注，这使得进行可靠的农业诊断非常困难。AgroGround数据集旨在解决这一问题，通过整合来自不同来源的农业VQA数据集的标签，建立了一个大规模的数据集，用于提高图像中的农业目标识别和定位能力。

Method: 本研究首先自动将八个农业VQA数据集的标签转换为植物病害和整物理体的注释，生成了794,850个指令示例。然后，通过结合定位和识别的指令对共享的视觉-语言模型进行微调。此外，还利用健康图像提供了否定监督，提高了模型在健康图像中的空预测性能，并引入了强化学习来提升病害区域的地基。

Result: 与单独进行定位微调相比，结合识别和定位指令可将识别准确性从51.8%提高到72.6%。同时，将两种格式的指令结合使用，联合准确性从19.2%提高到43.3%，同时保持相似的定位效果。健康图片的空预测率提高到了95.0%，并且通过强化学习进一步改进了病害区域的地基。

Conclusion: AgroGround数据集在基准测试中表现良好，特别是在病害区域的地基方面超过了它的标注教师。此外，该研究为联合身份和定位评估以及健康图像的放弃行为提供了一个基准。

Abstract: Agricultural visual models are typically evaluated for either recognition or localization, but reliable diagnosis requires identifying what is present and localizing the evidence. Agricultural visual question answering (VQA) datasets carry rich semantic labels but rarely link them to image regions, and adding such annotations by hand is costly at scale. We introduce AgroGround, a large-scale dataset for grounded agricultural recognition: identifying plant diseases and other agricultural targets and localizing their image regions. An automated pipeline converts the labels of eight agricultural VQA datasets into annotations for disease lesions and whole objects, producing 794,850 instruction examples. Healthy images provide negative supervision for disease queries, teaching the model to return empty predictions. We fine-tune a shared vision-language model on known-target grounding instructions combined with instructions requiring both recognition and localization. We evaluate predicted identities, regions, joint correctness, and healthy-image abstention on 1,480 human-verified images disjoint from all training data. Grounding-only fine-tuning reduces recognition accuracy from 51.8\% to 29.1\%, while adding recognition-and-localization instructions raises it to 72.6\%. With images and annotations held fixed, combining the two formats raises joint accuracy from 19.2\% to 43.3\% at comparable grounding. Healthy negatives raise abstention on healthy images to 95.0\%, and reinforcement learning improves lesion-level grounding. The resulting 2B model exceeds its annotation teacher in grounding F1 on our benchmark and on the external PlantSeg test set. AgroGround establishes a benchmark for grounded agricultural recognition, measuring joint correctness of identity and localization along with abstention on healthy images. The code is available at https://github.com/AB-Abdulla/AgroGround.

</details>


### [52] [Video2World: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Interactive World Modeling from Embodied Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04432)
*Jinzhou Tang,Zijun Zhang,Jing Yang,Yuchen Yan,Kun Zhou,Lingjun Mao,Ruobing Han,Jinglin Cao,Wenpeng Xu,Lukun He,Minghao Fu,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究通过前沿基础模型和编程代理自动化从真实世界观察构建互动模拟器的过程，引入Video2World基准测试，评估系统在几何保真度、动态保真度和功能正确性方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了自动化从真实世界观察构建互动模拟器的过程，减轻对人工环境构建和校准的依赖。

Method: 定义了从视频到模拟的自主过程，并通过评估九个前沿编程代理系统来衡量其效果。

Result: 初步结果显示，从5%提高到超过15%的成功率，但与人工辅助重建之间仍有显著差距。且视觉保真度更高的世界不总是具有更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 证明了使用具备感知能力和物理推理能力的前沿模型来进行视频到模拟转换具有潜力，但仍需克服实际应用中的挑战。

Abstract: Building interactive simulators from real-world observations is a promising way to scale embodied data, but current pipelines still rely heavily on manual environment construction and calibration. We study whether frontier foundation models and coding agents can automate this process end to end. We formulate \emph{autonomous video-to-simulation} as a software engineering task in which an agent observes an embodied video, constructs the corresponding simulated environment and robot behavior, and iteratively refines the result through execution feedback. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{Video2World}, a benchmark comprising 222 reconstruction instances derived from 189 robot and human demonstration videos. Video2World measures reconstructed worlds along geometric fidelity, dynamic fidelity, and functional correctness, capturing spatial perception, physical reasoning, and executable interaction. Evaluating 9 frontier coding-agent systems reveals a sharp improvement in Task success beginning with Claude Opus 5, rising from below 5\% to over 15\%, while substantial gaps to human-assisted reconstruction remain. We further find that worlds that look better could work worse: better visual fidelity does not always lead to higher task success. This echoes the broader gap between perceptual realism and factual correctness observed in generative models.

</details>


### [53] [Multi-Crop Leaf Disease Recognition: A Unified Benchmark and Cross-Region Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04456)
*Rosemary Nalwanga,Sebastian Bunda,Godliver Owomugisha,Luuk Spreeuwers,Estefania Talavera Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文引入了MLD多作物叶片疾病数据集，涵盖了18种作物和56种作物疾病类，通过评估协议研究跨区域适应性，并通过层级建模提高了疾病识别的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型在单一实验室数据集上表现出近乎完美的精度，但在实际多区域情况下表现不佳。因此，本文旨在构建一个多区域基准数据集，以评估模型在不同区域的应用和改进其跨区域适应性。

Method: 本文构建了MLD数据集，包括六个公共作物疾病数据集，集合了18种作物和56种疾病类。同时，制定了单源和多源的标准化评估协议，并通过层级建模指导疾病预测。

Result: 实验结果显示，在单一数据集上的深度学习模型对新数据集的表现极为不佳，但在使用合并数据集的模型中，通过层级建模提升了跨区域识别疾病的效果，取得了约26.76个百分点的改进。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MLD数据集和评估标准为研究跨区域适应性提供了基础，层级建模在一定程度上解决了跨区域适应性的问题，但需要更广泛的多样性和覆盖范围以进一步提升模型性能。

Abstract: Deep learning models for crop leaf disease recognition routinely report near-perfect accuracy yet are typically trained and evaluated on a single dataset collected under controlled laboratory conditions, leaving their behavior under realistic cross-region domain shift poorly understood. We introduce MLD (Multi-crop Leaf Disease) dataset, a unified multi-region benchmark that combines six public crop-disease datasets from the USA, Asia, and Africa into a shared hierarchical taxonomy spanning 18 crops, 56 crop-disease classes (including one healthy class per crop) making 167,427 images. We define standardized single-source and pooled multi-source evaluation protocols that explicitly probe cross-region generalization. We also investigate whether exploiting the inherent crop-to-disease dependency via a hierarchical formulation (HiLeaD) that conditions disease prediction on the predicted crop improves recognition under cross-region shift. Under the HiLeaD, the model trained on PlantVillage achieves 99.07% in-domain disease F1 but collapses to 12.88% when tested on PlantDoc, exposing a severe cross-region domain gap. The model trained on the pooled MLD dataset partly recovers cross-region disease F1 from 12.88% to 39.64% on PlantDoc (HiLeaD), achieving a 26.76 percentage point improvement. The hierarchical formulation provides a consistent additional gain, ranging from 1.71 to 4.88 percentage points in disease F1 over the flat baseline under the MLD dataset indicating that progress in this area is currently limited more by data coverage and diversity than by model design.

</details>


### [54] [RPFQ-ViT: Rotated Phase-Frame Quantization for Extremely Low-Bit Weights in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04457)
*Mengyuan Fan,Bokai Huang,JiaMing Pan,Xiaokun Yuan,Peizhuang Cong,Zhewen Tan,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RPFQ-ViT 使用旋转相平面量化通道组合，以减少 ViTs 的存储和推理成本，同时保持投影方向。在多个模型上进行实验，结果显示了良好的准确性和压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 ViTs 存在高维度的线性投影和注意力计算带来的高存储和推断成本问题，而常规的实值标量码本由于与变换器投影的方向几何不匹配，导致精度严重下降。因此，需要一个更有效的量化方法来解决这些问题。

Method: RPFQ-ViT 方法通过在二维相平面上量化成对的通道，让低位数码本能够更好地保持投影方向，同时通过轻量级的缩放恢复幅度。这种方法不需要修改标准的实值注意、归一化或激活计算图。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 上，RPFQ-ViT-B/16 达到了 79.33% 的 Top-1 和 94.48% 的 Top-5，Swin-T 达到了 79.30% 的 Top-1 和 94.79% 的 Top-5，DeiT-S 达到了 77.41% 的 Top-1 和 93.11% 的 Top-5。通过 2 位打包权重，模型大小相对于 FP32 减小了约 5.4-7.1 倍，端到端的设备内延迟减少了 1.4-1.6 倍。

Conclusion: RPFQ-ViT 为极端低位数 ViTs 提供了在准确度、压缩和实际移动部署方面的良好权衡。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance on image recognition and mobile vision applications, but their high-dimensional linear projections and attention computations still impose substantial storage and inference costs. Extremely low-bit quantization is a promising solution, yet ViTs often suffer severe accuracy degradation because conventional real-valued scalar codebooks are poorly matched to the directional geometry of Transformer projections. We present RPFQ-ViT, a Rotated Phase-Frame Quantization method that quantizes paired channels in two-dimensional phase planes, enabling low-bit codes to better preserve projection directions while recovering magnitude with lightweight scaling. RPFQ-ViT serves as a drop-in QAT replacement for nn.Linear and does not modify the standard real-valued attention, normalization, or activation computation graph. On ImageNet-1K, RPFQ-ViT-B/16 reaches 79.33% Top-1 / 94.48% Top-5 under W2/A4, Swin-T reaches 79.30% Top-1 / 94.79% Top-5 under W2/A8, and DeiT-S reaches 77.41% Top-1 / 93.11% Top-5 under W2/A8. Ablations, phase-geometry analysis, and direction-preservation metrics show that channel pairing, learnable rotation, phase-anchor learning, and residual phase refinement each improve quantization quality. We further deploy RPFQ-ViT image-classification models on native iOS and Android runtime stacks; with 2-bit packed weights, model size shrinks by roughly $5.4$-$7.1\times$ relative to FP32 and end-to-end on-device latency drops by $1.4$-$1.6\times$. All ImageNet results trained in our codebase use a matched 300-epoch recipe and are reported as mean accuracies over three independent runs. These results show that RPFQ-ViT provides a favorable trade-off among accuracy, compression, and practical mobile deployment for extremely low-bit ViTs.

</details>


### [55] [Homogeneous Semantic Alignment and Hierarchical Expert Routing for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04499)
*Erjian Zhang,Jiayuan Ma,Liejun Wang,Yikemaiti Sataer,Xiaoming Tao,Zhiqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的两阶段Homogeneous Semantic Alignment and Hierarchical Expert Routing (HSA-HER)框架，旨在有效消除跨模态分布偏移并动态激活专家网络，对多源证据进行针对性挖掘和语义重建，以实现医学影像到诊断文本的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学影像报告生成方法在处理跨模态交互和外部先验结合方面的进步，但由于潜在表示的分布偏移和异构信息的刚性耦合问题，难以精确捕捉影像中的微小异常。

Method: HSA-HER框架首先在潜在空间中引入了显式的同态分布约束，以消除视觉和文本特征之间的跨模态分布偏移，从而提取出与疾病精确对齐的净化视觉特征作为语义锚点。第二阶段利用基于这些疾病语义锚点的分层专家路由机制，动态激活专家网络对多源证据进行针对性挖掘和语义重建，适配性分配融合权重。

Result: 在三个主流基准数据集上的广泛实验中，HSA-HER框架表现出优越的性能，能够准确描绘复杂的影像细节和关键诊断信息。

Conclusion: HSA-HER框架为医学影像报告生成提供了一种有效的方法，能够更好地应对影像与文本之间的跨模态挑战，为临床决策支持和减轻医生工作负担提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) aims to convert medical images into diagnostic texts to assist in clinical decision-making and alleviate the workload of physicians. Although existing methods have made extensive progress in cross-modal interaction and the incorporation of external priors, the distribution shift of underlying representations and the undifferentiated rigid coupling of heterogeneous information cause weak visual abnormality cues to be easily diluted by massive text priors and generation inertia during decoding. To overcome this bottleneck, inspired by cognitive science, we propose a novel two-stage Homogeneous Semantic Alignment and Hierarchical Expert Routing (HSA-HER) framework. First, the model introduces an explicit homogeneous distribution constraint in the underlying latent space to effectively eliminate the cross-modal distribution shift between visual and textual features, thereby extracting purified visual features as semantic anchors that accurately align with diseases. Second, for heterogeneous clinical evidence composed of visual features, local entities, and global retrievals, we design a hierarchical expert routing mechanism guided by these disease semantic anchors. This mechanism abandons the undifferentiated rigid coupling paradigm. Specifically, it dynamically activates expert networks to perform targeted mining and semantic reconstruction on multi-source evidence, and adaptively allocates fusion weights. Extensive experiments on three mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSA-HER achieves state-of-the-art performance, accurately depicting complex imaging details and key diagnostic information.

</details>


### [56] [Localization Lens for Improving Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04502)
*Hasan Farooq,Murtaza Taj,Mehwish Nasim,Arif Mahmood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种增强机制，通过改进数据、架构和对齐三个层面的Med-VLM，以提升医学图像中的解剖结构理解和空间定位，实验表明这能提高医学视觉问答任务的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Med-VLM在理解和定位医学图像中的解剖结构和空间位置方面存在不足，这对医学决策至关重要。因此，提出了一种改进方法来提升Med-VLM在相关任务中的表现。

Method: 该方法包括引入专家验证的定位镜头以提供丰富的解剖和位置上下文，通过在模型架构中使用像素移位来筛选和细化表示，增强空间信息处理并保持解剖连续性，以及结合解耦对比损失以更好地对齐定位镜头表示和文本特征。

Result: 在广泛的医疗视觉问答数据集上的评估显示，这种方法在不同的Med-VLM架构中提高了以定位驱动的表现。进一步的分析表明，解剖和空间推理的改进可以将Med-VQA的准确性提高6.2%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法是一种通用的、可以无缝集成到现有Med-VLM管道的方法，该方法已经在GitHub上开源，将促进医学视觉理解领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in clinical tasks. However, they often struggle to understand anatomical structures and spatial positioning, which are crucial for medical reasoning. To address this, we propose a localization-aware enhancement to the Med-VLM pipeline, introducing improvements at three levels: data,architecture, and alignment. First, we introduce localization lens, a set of expert-validated representations that provide richer anatomical and positional context. However, as these representations increase input complexity, we integrate pixel shuffle within the model architecture to filter and refine representations, enhancing spatial information processing while preserving anatomical continuity. Lastly, to effectively align the localization lens representations with textual features, we incorporate decoupled contrastive loss (DCL) alongside the standard loss function. This ensures better feature discrimination and robustness, particularly in data limited medical settings. Through extensive evaluations on medical visual question answering (Med-VQA) datasets, we show that our methodology improves localization-driven performance across different Med-VLM architectures. Our analysis of localization-based questions further reveals that improvements in anatomy and spatial reasoning directly enhance the overall accuracy of Med-VQA upto 6.2%. The proposed approach is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into existing Med-VLM pipelines. The dataset, code, and trained models will be made publicly available at https://github.com/CVLABLUMS/localizationlens.

</details>


### [57] [EgoExo-Next:Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Visual-Option Next-State and Cross-View Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04506)
*Yutong Li,Molin Wang,Xiaotong Li,Yanyan Fang,Daoguo Dong,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoExo-Next是一个包含2,503个人工策划的四选一问题的基准，旨在评估视觉-语言模型在动态视觉状态推理中的能力。研究显示，尽管模型在各个子任务上的表现各异，整体上与人类表现仍有较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准主要集中在语义事件理解、时间关系或已观察视图之间的对应关系，而EgoExo-Next特别关注模型在未来视觉状态的直接推理能力。

Method: 构建包含六个公共数据集来源的2,503个人工策划的问题集，涵盖四个互相关联的子任务：第一视角下一步状态预测、双向第一视角-外视角状态对应、外视角下一步状态预测以及它们的组合。

Result: 对专有、开源和空间推理VLMs进行了广泛的评估，发现最佳模型平均准确率为43.81%，而人类的准确率为98.55%。特别是在组合第一视角到外视角的任务中，性能下降最为明显。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，现有的VLMs在动态视觉状态推理方面仍然存在明显的局限性，特别是在需要进行时间进展和跨视图推理的情况下。EgoExo-Next基准已经公开可用。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated for egocentric and cross-view video reasoning, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on semantic event understanding, temporal relations, or correspondence between already observed views, leaving their ability to reason directly about future visual states underexplored. We introduce EgoExo-Next, a visual-option benchmark for dynamic visual-state reasoning, where models must identify how an observed action trajectory subsequently appears rather than predict only an action label or textual description. EgoExo-Next contains 2,503 human-curated four-choice questions from six public egocentric and ego--exo video sources and comprises four interconnected subtasks that evaluate egocentric next-state prediction, bidirectional ego--exo state correspondence, exocentric next-state prediction, and their composition in Ego-to-Exo Next-State. Extensive evaluation of proprietary, open-source, and spatial reasoning VLMs reveals a substantial human--model gap, with the best model achieving 43.81\% average accuracy compared with 98.55\% for humans, and the largest degradation occurring on the composed Ego-to-Exo task. These results suggest that current VLMs remain substantially limited in dynamic visual-state reasoning, particularly when temporal progression and cross-view reasoning must be composed. The benchmark is publicly available at \url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/yutongli2024/EgoExo-Next}.

</details>


### [58] [TAME:Topology-Aware Text-Driven Motion Editing across Heterogeneous Humanoid Skeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04529)
*Qichen Zheng,Siyuan Yang,Chong Wang,Jun Liu,Shijian Lu,Alex Kot,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAME（Topology-Aware Motion Editor）通过一种流匹配转换器编辑人物骨架变化，采用关节和帧的令牌表示动作，并利用骨骼、时间以及文本的交叉注意力机制。为使框架关注遵循每个角色的层级结构，替代全面关节关注，使用了TopoConstraint Skeletal Propagation (TCSP) 限制关注到骨架邻接矩阵的一跳血缘邻域。此外，引入编辑焦点表示对齐 (EFRA) 策略，专注于编辑相关的关节-时间令牌。TAME 在 MotionFix 和 TopoMotionFix 数据集上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常针对固定骨骼拓扑，限制了它们在不同关节数量和骨骼层次的动画工作流程中的应用。该研究的动机是开发一种拓扑感知的运动编辑方法，以支持不同骨骼拓扑角色的运动编辑。

Method: TAME（Topology-Aware Motion Editor）采用流匹配转换器来编辑不同拓扑结构的人形骨架上的运动。它将动作表示为每关节、每帧的令牌，并通过骨骼、时间、文本交叉注意机制建模关节间的相互作用。为确保关注与每个角色的层级结构相符，TAME 使用了一种TopoConstraint Skeletal Propagation (TCSP) 技术来限制关注范围。此外，还引入了创新的编辑焦点表示对齐 (EFRA)，专门在编辑相关的关节-时间令牌上对准学生特征与教师特征。

Result: TAME 在 MotionFix 和 TopoMotionFix 数据集上的性能表现出色，优于之前的方法。在编辑定位和源运动保持方面，TAME 实现了卓越的效果，在针对未见过的骨架进行运动编辑时也表现得非常可靠。

Conclusion: TAME 提供了一种多功能的运动编辑框架，适用于不同骨骼拓扑的角色，显著提高了运动编辑的准确性和适应性。

Abstract: Text-driven motion editing modifies an existing motion sequence according to a text instruction while preserving the content of the source motion. Existing methods are typically built for a single, fixed skeletal topology, which limits their use in animation pipelines where characters differ in joint count and skeletal hierarchy. We present Topology-Aware Motion Editor (TAME), a flow-matching transformer that edits motions on humanoid skeletons of varying topology. TAME represents motion as per-joint, per-frame tokens and models interactions among joints, across frames, and with the text instruction through skeletal, temporal, and text cross-attention layers. To make the skeletal attention follow each character's hierarchy, TAME replaces full joint attention with Topology-Constrained Skeletal Propagation (TCSP), which restricts attention to one-hop kinematic neighbors in the skeleton's adjacency matrix. We further introduce Edit-Focused Representation Alignment (EFRA), a self-distilled representation alignment strategy that aligns student features with cleaner EMA-teacher features exclusively on edit-relevant joint-time tokens, making edits faithful to the instruction. To make this setting trainable and comparable, we construct TopoMotionFix, a multi-topology extension of MotionFix with seen- and unseen-topology evaluation protocols. TAME outperforms previous methods in edit alignment and source preservation on MotionFix and reliably edits motions on unseen skeletons in TopoMotionFix.

</details>


### [59] [FASTER: Fast Adjoint Stochastic Transport for Endpoint Refinement in Reward-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04538)
*Yimiao Zhou,Zejia Zhong,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FASTER在保持源内容和视觉可信度的前提下，在测试时间优化指定奖励，通过引入一个小网络进行低成本编辑，同时使用预训练模型和奖励模型提供反馈，从而实现快速编辑，改善了SD3的四项目标指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进奖励导向的图像编辑方法，在保持源信息和视觉合理性的前提下，优化指定奖励。解决现有方法中预训练生成过程导致的昂贵调整执行问题。

Method: FASTER通过建立一个理论框架，联合考虑奖励、源内容保存和预训练先验偏好。基于此框架，FASTER训练一个小型网络进行经济有效的编辑，且预训练和奖励模型可以给候选输出提供反馈。该方法通过重用每个候选输出及其反馈来减少重复采样和监督查询。

Result: FASTER在SD3中所有评估方法中处于领先地位，尤其是在四个目标指标方面。与优化控制沿预训练生成轨迹的评估基线相比，FASTER在Stable Diffusion 3中实现了最高至6.91倍的速度提升，在Stable Diffusion 1.5中实现了最高至24.14倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: FASTER通过高效的小型网络训练提高了测试时间的编辑速度，在多个评估指标上表现突出。

Abstract: Reward-guided image editing at test time seeks to improve a specified reward while preserving source content and visual plausibility. Many existing approaches optimize candidates through pretrained generation processes, making repeated adjustment depend on costly large-model execution and, in some cases, backbone backpropagation. We develop a theoretical framework that jointly accounts for reward, source preservation, and pretrained-prior preferences, allowing the desired output distribution to be specified separately from the dynamics used to realize it. Based on this framework, we introduce FASTER, which trains a small network for each source and objective to perform inexpensive editing, while pretrained and reward models provide feedback on candidate outputs. By reusing each candidate and its feedback across multiple small-network updates, FASTER reduces repeated sampling and supervision queries without placing the pretrained generative backbone inside the inner optimization loop. On SD3, FASTER leads all four target metrics and several validation metrics among the evaluated methods. Compared with the evaluated baseline that optimizes controls along pretrained generation trajectories, FASTER achieves editing-time speedups of up to \({6.91\times}\) on Stable Diffusion 3 and \({24.14\times}\) on Stable Diffusion 1.5.

</details>


### [60] [EagleDepth: Efficient Fine-Grained Depth Estimation via Pixel Diffusion Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04554)
*Bowen Chai,Tianbao Zhang,Shuyu Wu,Dexin Zuo,Zhaoxin Fan,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EagleDepth 提出了一种高效框架，用于高分辨率单目深度估计，通过结合潜在扩散模型的几何先验与精细像素空间生成，实现端到端训练，具有更快的推理速度和更好的细节保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在使用潜在空间建模和 VAE 重建时会牺牲几何细节，且从潜在代码解码会带来大量推理开销。EagleDepth 旨在解决这些问题，通过在像素空间直接生成最终深度图，保留深度感知的潜在表示，同时允许潜在支路在较低分辨率下运行。

Method: EagleDepth 采用了一种端到端的训练方法，首先通过配对的 RGB-深度监督微调预训练的潜在扩散模型；然后适应预训练的像素扩散解码器 PiD，基于学习到的特征预测深度。培训自 1024 像素分辨率开始，逐级扩展到 4K。

Result: 在五个常用深度估计数据集和高分辨率 Synth4K 数据集上，EagleDepth 实现了最先进的深度估计性能，具有更快的推理速度和更好的细节保真度。

Conclusion: EagleDepth 通过结合几何先验和像素空间生成，提供了一种高效的高分辨率单目深度估计系统，显著改善了深度估计质量和推理效率。

Abstract: Recovering detailed geometry from high-resolution images is critical for precise perception of the surroundings and objects. However, existing methods which use latent-space modeling and VAE reconstruction can compromise geometric details. Furthermore, decoding from latent codes introduces substantial inference overhead. To address those issues, we present EagleDepth, an efficient framework for high-resolution monocular depth estimation that combines the geometric priors of latent diffusion with fine-grained pixel-space generation. Our key idea is to retain depth-aware latent representations as guidance while generating the final depth map directly in pixel space. We train the latent and pixel components sequentially: first, we fine-tune a pretrained latent diffusion model using paired RGB--depth supervision; then, we adapt a pretrained pixel diffusion decoder, PiD, to predict depth conditioned on the learned features. Training of the pixel component starts at 1024 resolution and continues across multiple resolutions up to 4K. The latent branch processes resized, lower-resolution RGB images, while the pixel branch generates depth at the target resolution, bypassing the original VAE decoder. This design preserves learned geometric knowledge without requiring the latent backbone to operate at the output resolution. On five commonly used depth estimation datasets and the high-resolution Synth4K dataset, our framework achieves state-of-the-art depth estimation performance, with faster inference and better preservation of fine structures and object boundaries.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [61] [Fine-Grained Emotion Classification from Mobile App Reviews: An Empirical Study with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03802)
*Quim Motger,Carlota Catot,Marc Oriol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用大规模语言模型进行应用程序评论的细粒度情感分类，采用多标签微调、二进制集成及不同的不平衡缓解策略，发现最佳的零样本提示策略在宏F1得分上表现出色，同时在低延迟下显著提高了罕见情感的分类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度情感分类对于移动应用评估和需求工程具有重要价值，而现有的自动细粒度情感提取研究较少。因此，本文旨在利用大规模语言模型探索其在这一领域中的潜力。

Method: 研究比较了多标签微调、二进制集成以及多种不平衡缓解策略（如权重重设、重抽样和生成数据扩充），并通过内生增量效用排名选择最佳的合成评测生成器和提示策略。

Result: 研究表明，仅编码器微调的表现明显逊于最好的零样本提示策略，后者在宏F1得分上的表现优于所有其他方法，尤其是在罕见情感分类方面的提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，大规模语言模型能够实现在应用程序评论中自动进行细粒度情感分类，尽管宏观F1得分并不高，但最佳的框架和缓解策略依赖于基础模型和方法形式。研究还提供了实验框架、合成语料库和微调检查点以供复现和使用。

Abstract: Context: Fine-grained emotion classification of mobile app reviews enables requirements engineering activities that go beyond polarity-based opinion mining, including emotionally informed issue prioritisation and feature-oriented feedback analysis. However, automatic fine-grained emotion extraction from app reviews remains understudied. Objectives: Building on a previously published annotation framework and human-labelled ground truth adapted from Plutchik's taxonomy, this paper investigates how large language models can be leveraged for automatic multi-label emotion classification under severe class imbalance. Methods: We compare encoder-only fine-tuning under multi-label and binary-ensemble formulations, decoder-only zero- and few-shot prompting across open-source and proprietary models, and a catalogue of imbalance mitigation strategies (loss reweighting, resampling, generative data augmentation), with the synthetic-review generator and prompting strategy selected via an intrinsic augmentation-utility ranking. Results: Fine-tuned encoders trail the best decoder-only few-shot prompting (macro-F1 0.642) by a wide margin at baseline (multi-label: 0.387; binary ensemble: 0.450); pairing the best multi-label encoder with generative data augmentation and positive-weighted loss closes most of this gap (+0.204) at up to three orders of magnitude lower inference latency than the decoders, with the largest gains on the rarest emotions, from undetected to gains of up to +0.501 F1. Conclusion: Large language models make fine-grained, multi-label emotion classification of app reviews feasible for requirements engineering pipelines, with modest macro-F1, and the best formulation and mitigation strategy are backbone- and formulation-dependent. We release the experimental pipeline, synthetic corpora, and fine-tuned checkpoints for replication and reuse.

</details>


### [62] [Same Output, Different Gold: Measuring How Reference Choice Moves a Multilingual Benchmark Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03825)
*Parth Kulshreshtha,Shivali Dalmia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过交换参考标准来重新衡量系统输出，发现评分结果变化显著，归因于一个未记录的聚合默认设置，该设置通常保留了一个标注者的信息，即使仲裁未触发。研究提出了参考标准敏感性的范围，并建议将其作为评分的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于当前评分方法主要关注一端——系统输出，而忽视了参考标准的质量，研究旨在通过改进方法论来评估评分的准确性。

Method: 使用了一个六语言基准数据集，该数据集包含两个独立标注者的标注记录，以及专家重注的一个样本。研究通过固定评分输出，交换参考标准，来重新评估系统表现。

Result: 研究结果显示，不同的参考标准对评分有显著影响。一个输出的得分变化了4.95 F1分数（95％置信区间为[3.23, 6.27]），另一个输出变化了2.00分（95% CI [0.26, 3.37]），最极端的情况下，变化达7.55分。这表明参考标准对评分结果有很大影响。

Conclusion: 研究发现了评分参考标准中的不确定性，并提出了一种计算参考标准敏感性的方法，建议将其纳入评分报告，以提高评分透明度和准确性。

Abstract: A benchmark score compares a system output against a reference, and methodological attention falls almost entirely on the first term. We measure the second. The retained annotation record of a six-language benchmark for personally identifiable information contains two independent annotator labellings, the aggregate shipped as gold, and a reviewer gold from independent expert re-annotation of a sample. Using it, we hold the scored output fixed and exchange the reference. The score moves by 4.95 F1 points for one output and 2.00 for the other (95% CIs [3.23, 6.27] and [0.26, 3.37]), and by 7.55 in the worst language. The comparison between two outputs moves as well: the paired interaction between reference and system is +2.95 points (CI [+1.97, +3.87]), survives correction for multiple testing, and changes one language's margin outright. The cause is an undocumented aggregation default that usually kept one annotator when adjudication did not fire, making the shipped reference a partial copy of an output being scored. We report the resulting reference-sensitivity band, show how to compute one from any retained annotation record, and argue that the quantity belongs beside the score.

</details>


### [63] [The Score Is Not the Structure: Brain Alignment and Cross-Lingual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03827)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 此类研究通过对比分析得出了在共享结构不存在或评测工具失效情况下，相似度分数的实际情况，提醒在解读这些分数时要谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章指出了在测量语料与脑结构或跨语言共享结构相似度时，评测工具的有效性以及统计单位选择的重要性。

Method: 文章设定了两种场景进行测试：第一，对不同语言测试探针模型；第二，语言模型匹配人脑响应。随后分析了不同条件下的得分变化，以及模型性能在不同条件下的具体表现。

Result: 结果显示，在一些最远的语言对中，探针模型的表现已经达到了随机水平；重新分析统计单位后发现在不同语言中模型得分的显著性减弱；匹配人脑响应可以提高模型得分，但得分提升的增量部分并非完全由脑响应决定；方向引导模型在多数语言中的表现显著优于随机方向，但未显示与语言距离的关联。

Conclusion: 文章建议在评估模型与脑结构或跨语言的共享结构相似度时，应考虑评测工具的有效性和统计方法的适用性。

Abstract: Researchers often support the claim that a model shares structure with the brain, or across languages, by reporting a similarity score. We ask what such a score reads when the shared structure is absent, or when the tool that measures it does not work. We check two settings, and in both the score is not what it appears. First, a probe trained to tell grammatical from ungrammatical sentences in one language transfers worse to more distant languages, the usual evidence for shared structure. But the probe itself gets worse along the same axis: in four of seventeen languages it performs at chance, so 64 of 272 language pairs are scored with a tool that does not work. Dropping those languages halves the strength of the relationship, but they are also the most distant, and this design cannot separate the two effects. Counting matters too: the same data give p = 0.0006 when the 272 pairs are treated as independent and p = 0.155 when the seventeen languages are, which is the correct unit. Second, training a language model to match human brain responses raises its similarity score from 0.10 to 0.34, against a ceiling of 0.54. A model trained on a target whose correspondence to the brain was destroyed still scores 0.31, so only 0.028 to 0.068 of the rise is specific to the brain. With no model at all, a destroyed target already sits at 0.204 from the real one, a floor that tracks the target's rank divided by the number of sentences. Finally, steering a language along its own direction works (+6.2 over a random direction in sixteen of seventeen languages) yet shows no effect that varies with language distance. Before asking whether a correspondence helps, ask how much of the score would survive without it.

</details>


### [64] [OncoNoteBERT: A Foundation Representation Model for Natural Language Processing of Real-World Outpatient Oncology Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03829)
*Wuraola Oyewusi,Eliana Vasquez Osorio,Goran Nenadic,Gareth Price*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过使用特定于肿瘤学的BERT变异体对特定UK肿瘤科门诊语料库进行微调和从零开始训练，评估了这些模型在肿瘤学术语识别和上下文预测中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改进针对现实世界肿瘤科门诊病历的专业术语处理、疗程描述及其他特殊标记，作者开发了特定于肿瘤学的BERT变体，并与从零训练和持续预训练的变体进行了对比。

Method: 使用了一个包含肺癌和头颈部癌症患者290,026份病历记录（21,564位患者）的英国数据集，分别对RadBERT和PathologyBERT进行了评估，同时对比了OncoNote-RadBERT和OncoNoteBERT。模型通过掩蔽语言建模损失和困惑度、分词碎片度度量、临床术语分词、掩蔽标记探针和探究性表示分析进行评估。

Result: 继续预训练产生的模型在零样本评估中显示了最佳的拟合度（2.10），而从零开始训练的OncoNoteBERT则在分词效率方面表现最佳。OncoNoteBERT在13个掩蔽标记探针中有12个提供了可行的预测。研究表明，继续适应和自定义分词提供了互补的益处，且在将相邻领域编码器应用于肿瘤学NLP之前，需要注意表示层设计。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，继续适应和自定义分词在肿瘤学NLP任务中具有重要意义，可以作为在邻近领域预训练的编码器应用于肿瘤学时的因素考虑。

Abstract: Real-world outpatient oncology notes contain specialised terminology, tumour staging expressions, treatment names, toxicity descriptions, and institution-specific de-identification markers that may not be represented efficiently by general biomedical or adjacent clinical language models. We developed and evaluated oncology-specific BERT-style encoders using a governed UK outpatient oncology corpus comprising 290,026 notes from 21,564 patients treated for lung and head-and-neck cancer. We compared RadBERT and PathologyBERT with two local strategies: OncoNote-RadBERT, produced by continued masked language model pretraining, and OncoNoteBERT, trained from scratch with an oncology WordPiece tokenizer. Models were evaluated using masked language modelling loss and perplexity on the validation set, tokenizer fragmentation metrics, clinical term tokenisation, masked-token probes, and exploratory representation analysis. Both external encoders fit the oncology corpus poorly in zero-shot evaluation (perplexity 113.04 for RadBERT; 2035.03 for PathologyBERT), while continued pretraining produced the strongest fit (2.10 for OncoNote-RadBERT). OncoNoteBERT achieved perplexity 2.83 but produced the most efficient tokenisation, with lower subword fertility and shorter normalised sequence length. It also returned a clinically acceptable prediction for 12 of 13 masked-token probes, compared with 7 of 13 for OncoNote-RadBERT. This divergence between corpus-level fit and masked-token performance was partly attributable to tokenizer fragmentation rather than learned semantics alone. Both locally developed models represented the institutional placeholder as a single learnable token. These findings show that continued adaptation and bespoke tokenisation provide complementary benefits, and that representation-layer design matters before adjacent-domain encoders are applied to oncology NLP.

</details>


### [65] [SYNLAT: Syntax-Aligned Text-Latent Compression for Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03839)
*Yifeng Zhao,Hongjun Yu,Shibo Wang,Yunjiao Zhou,Zixiao Zhu,Zhipeng Ning,Kezhi Mao,Junlang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynLat 是一种新的文本潜在 CoT 框架，通过非重叠的语法对齐单元（SAUs）将压缩边界与句法结构对齐，从而在保持答案关键信息的同时减少输出令牌成本，特别是在强压缩条件下，效果更为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算受限的情况下，需要一种方法减少长链推理（CoT）的输出令牌成本，同时保持答案的关键信息，特别是针对长链推理段落。

Method: SynLat 通过非重叠的语法对齐单元（SAUs）将压缩边界与句法结构对齐，构建一个教师和学生模型系统，其中教师采用答案条件的方法构建逐步的“保持”和“潜在”目标，学生根据压缩条件生成混合推理。

Result: SynLat 在所有 12 个任务组聚合中均与最强基线匹配或超过，在三个压缩级别中报告的实际压缩率下，严格领先于其他方法。对于 Qwen3-8B/14B，总体收益分别达到 3.6/2.6 点在 MEDIUM 压缩和 7.0/5.5 点在 HIGH 压缩，特别是在强压缩条件下，尤其是在长链推理段落。

Conclusion: SynLat 证明了通过语法对齐单元（SAUs）将压缩边界与句法结构对齐的有效性，能够在减少输出令牌成本的同时保持答案关键信息，特别是在强压缩条件下显示出明显优势。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) traces impose substantial output-token costs. Under constrained budgets, compression must preserve answer-critical information, making boundary placement central. Token-level and fixed-length boundaries can fragment coherent spans such as phrases, formulas, and local derivations, whereas step-level boundaries can bind content requiring different compression actions. We introduce SynLat, a text-latent CoT framework that aligns compression boundaries with syntactic structure through non-overlapping Syntax-Aligned Units (SAUs). An answer-conditioned Teacher constructs progressive KEEP/LATENT targets for a single compression-conditioned Student, which generates mixed reasoning from only the question and requested compression level at inference. Across two Qwen3 Student scales, Standard-CoT and Long-CoT groups, and three compression levels, SynLat matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in all 12 task-group aggregates and strictly leads in 11 under the reported achieved-CR selection protocol. Overall gains reach 3.6/2.6 points at MEDIUM and 7.0/5.5 points at HIGH for Qwen3-8B/14B, with larger advantages under stronger compression, particularly on Long-CoT groups.

</details>


### [66] [When Evidence Changes: Evaluating Memory Repair and Re-reading in Language-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03902)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了撤销或替换支持代理推论事实的文档时，应选择修复记忆还是重读当前证据。研究重点关注ICU记录中的药物和问题列表任务，通过评估不同的记忆处理策略来确定它们的成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型面对文档撤销和替换时的处理策略差异导致了代理记忆成本的衡量标准不一致，因此需要一个用于评估不同策略成本的标准化方法。

Method: 通过在公共ICU记录上进行证据修订评估，研究了撤销、替换和控制事件下的完整重读与来源过滤重读在两种7B模型中的成本差异。同时在小规模的开发扫面中对无关任务文档进行了添加。

Result: 本地修复比重建使用了5-10倍少的修订令牌，但在保留条件下的每个记忆管道成本至少是完全重读的两倍。在记录达到约10,000个标记时，记忆的累积成本在2-14次使用后低于完全重读。源过滤重读是成本最低的策略。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，代理记忆在证据修订后的成本需要与来源过滤重读在整个管道的成本进行对比评估。

Abstract: When documents supporting an agent's derived facts are revoked or replaced, should it repair memory or re-read current evidence? We introduce an evidence-revision evaluation on medication- and problem-list tasks from public ICU records. Under revocation, replacement and control events, we compare full and source-filtered re-reading with caching, rebuilding and graph-local repair across two 7B models. Memory is supplied in full without retrieval, and costs include ingest, revision and every use. On short records, local repair uses 5-10$\times$ fewer revision tokens than rebuilding, yet every memory pipeline costs at least twice full re-reading in held-out conditions. In a small pre-specified development sweep, adding task-ineligible documents extended records to about 10,000 tokens; at that length, memory's mean cumulative cost fell below full re-reading's after 2-14 uses, partly through truncated extraction, while source-filtered re-reading remained cheapest. In the replacement study, none of the four primary confirmatory tests reached statistical significance. These results show why the cost of agent memory after evidence revision must be assessed against source-filtered re-reading over the full pipeline.

</details>


### [67] [General Decision Models: Benchmarking and Insights Beyond Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03935)
*Feiyu Duan,Jiayu Lin,Jia Wang,Jun Xiang,Jialiang Wu,Xinnong Zhang,Hanqi Yan,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过JEVal基准测试了通用决策模型（如Jev）和生成型语言模型在结构化判断和选择任务中的表现，发现通用决策模型擅长处理可从现有证据中解决的问题，但当决策需要专门知识或准确的不确定性估计时表现较弱。在动态系统中，快速局部决策会因错误累积而降低整体可靠性。大型社会模拟中，决策模型在个体响应预测上接近生成型语言模型，但在用户画像方面较弱，误差和偏差较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估通用决策模型在结构化判断和选择任务中的性能，特别关注它们在复杂系统和长期互动中的表现。希望通过构建JEVal基准和比较多种模型配置，揭示这些决策模型的局限性和优势。

Method: 研究构建了JEVal基准，包含11,257个实例，覆盖36个数据集和10个应用领域，评估了25种模型配置，包含通用决策模型和生成型语言模型。使用定性和定量方法，分析了模型在不同场景下的表现。

Result: 研究结果表明，通用决策模型在可从现有证据中解决的问题上表现出色，但在需要专业知识或准确不确定性估计的任务中表现较弱；在动态系统中，快速局部决策降低了系统的可靠性；在大规模社会模拟中，模型在个体响应预测上接近生成型语言模型，但在用户画像方面存在不足。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了InnerJev-4B和InnerJev-27B，这是一种将开源语言模型自身推理内化的自我蒸馏方法，能够在保持性能的同时显著减少推理时间。

Abstract: General decision models, such as Jev, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to LLMs for structured judgment and selection. But what kinds of decisions can these models reliably make, and how does their behavior change when individual decisions are composed into larger systems? To study this, we introduce JEVal, a bilingual benchmark comprising 11,257 instances from 36 datasets across 10 application domains, and evaluate 25 model configurations spanning general decision models and generative LLMs. Our results show that (1) general decision models are most competitive when decisions can be resolved from available evidence, but weaken when they require specialist knowledge or faithful uncertainty estimation: they can often identify the most likely outcome while substantially overstating its probability. (2) In more dynamic and realistic systems involving long-horizon, multi-step interactions, the advantages of fast local decision making are offset by reliability failures at the system level. on $τ$-bench, faster local decisions reduce median episode time but lower task success as decision errors accumulate over long trajectories. (3) In large-scale social simulation, decision models approach strong generative LLMs on individual response prediction at substantially lower inference cost, yet remain weaker in user profiling and exhibit larger aggregate estimation errors and systematic bias. Finally, we propose InnerJev-4B and InnerJev-27B, which internalize an open-weight LLM's own reasoning into a single-pass first-token decision through Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation, with InnerJev-27B performing on par with Jev on JEVal while answering a typical query in about 0.1 s.

</details>


### [68] [A Step Towards Forgetting: Optimiser History and the Loss of Answer Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03940)
*Valeria Ruscio,Seth Nabarro,Keiran Thompson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在微调过程中，尽管当前梯度促使语言模型保留已学习的答案概率，但动量会使模型在更新时也携带早期计算的梯度，从而可能导致遗忘。分隔旧任务损失为答案混淆和答案集外的概率泄漏，发现答案质量随训练下降，但答案间的区分能力通常提高。通过调整动量和历史记忆，可以减少遗忘并提升旧任务的最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究优化器记忆如何影响语言模型旧任务的答案处理，在微调过程中模型可能忘记已有知识，探索这种遗忘的具体机制。

Method: 通过分析不同语言模型家族在使用Adam优化器下的微调表现，分离对旧任务损失的贡献为答案混淆和答案集外的概率泄漏，利用年龄权重的历史记忆分解Adam更新，设计干预措施调整优化器的记忆。

Result: 发现答案质量随训练下降，但答案间的区分能力通常提高。动量中的历史记忆对遗忘起重要作用，特别是来自新任务的较老梯度，影响旧任务答案的保留。干预措施如重置动量可以改善旧任务的最终性能。

Conclusion: 优化器的过去记忆可以破坏已学习的行为，即使当前梯度试图保留这些行为。通过合理管理优化器的记忆，可以减少这种影响，从而提高模型的长期性能。

Abstract: During fine-tuning, a language model can assign less probability to previously learned answers even when the current gradient acts to preserve that probability. With momentum, each update also carries gradients computed at earlier model states, and these stored contributions can push the model in the opposite direction. We investigate how this optimiser memory contributes to forgetting by separating old-task loss into confusion among its answers and leakage of probability outside the answer set. Across three language-model families, answer mass consistently declines while discrimination among old answers usually improves: the model becomes less likely to produce answers that it can still distinguish correctly. Decomposing Adam updates reveals opposing contributions to this loss of answer mass. Over training, accumulated history favours leakage, while the current gradient opposes it. Resolving history by age shows that the harmful contributions come mainly from older gradients of the new task, whereas recent gradients tend to protect the old answers. Changes in history's effect are dominated by its orientation relative to the old-task gradient. Interventions that reset momentum while matching the initial update norm establish that stored history affects retention, with state-dependent immediate effects and lower final old-task loss over longer Adam continuations, mainly through recovered answer mass. Finally, integration along finite updates shows that most sampled large loss increases are captured by local projections, while curvature along history amplifies some events. Together, these findings reveal how an optimiser's memory can erode learned behaviour even as its current gradient acts to preserve it.

</details>


### [69] [BAIBAICHUCHU at the NTCIR-19 FinArg-3 Task: When Is Maximum Possible Profit Predictable from Investor Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03962)
*Zong-Han Bai,Po-Yen Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该团队参与NTCIR-19 FinArg-3社交媒体子任务，基于最大可能收益排名中文投资者帖子。尽管采用了三轨集成方法，但仍发现了一些问题，如一个长期规则导致了不准确的结果，并且对评分的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过参与NTCIR-19 FinArg-3，评估并改进中文投资者帖子的排名方法，以更好地利用最大可能收益作为评估标准。

Method: 采用了三轨集成方法，包括词汇特征、Fine-tuned MacBERT排名器和LLM裁判。还对排名过程进行了事后审计和分解，以分析不同因素的影响。

Result: 三轨集成方法在组内开发评估中的得分为0.734，最佳官方运行得分为0.517。通过纠正长期规则的应用，虽然没有改变准确性但提高了开发准确性。分解后发现，预发布波动与特定观察期的后续收益有一定关联，并有助于排序。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在评估投资帖子时应考虑文本、市场环境、历史波动性、时间窗口以及配对组成。

Abstract: The BAIBAICHUCHU team participated in the Social Media Subtask of NTCIR-19 FinArg-3, ranking Chinese investor posts by Maximum Possible Profit (MPP). A three-track ensemble of lexical features, a FinArg-2-pre-finetuned MacBERT ranker, and an LLM judge reaches 0.734 in post-grouped development evaluation, but our best official run scores 0.517. All twelve submitted runs lie between 0.4598 and 0.5402, and our 26 unanimous three-track pairs score 0.500.
  A post-hoc audit finds that the submitted judge applied a long-only rule to bearish posts although MPP is stance-aware. Correcting it changes 28 of 87 official predictions without changing accuracy, yet lowers development accuracy from 0.680 to 0.622: a regime-specific semantic shortcut improved validation fit.
  We decompose ranking into directional text, volatility and horizon, and pairwise margin. A running-extremum model predicts $σ\sqrt{T}$ scaling, observed ex post in 502 price-aligned posts from a separate July 2026 collection. Pre-posting volatility scaled by the post-specific observation horizon, $σ_{\mathrm{pre},20}\sqrt{N_i}$, is associated with a later truncated-horizon MPP proxy (Spearman $ρ=0.320$; ticker-cluster 95% CI [0.133,0.466]) and correctly orders 61.4% of unequal-outcome pairs. Transferred text is nearly uncorrelated with the July outcome ($ρ=0.055$) and adds little conditional on this score and stance. Development reliability rises from about 0.60 to 0.93 as the labeled MPP gap widens. An earlier ERAI result of 0.6207 rules out universal unpredictability as a simple explanation. Evaluation should jointly consider text, market regime, historical volatility, horizon, and pair composition.

</details>


### [70] [Do Language Models Need a Trainable Input Embedding Table? Fixed Minimal Token Codes at 1.7B-Class Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04002)
*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究人员探讨了独立可调整的词汇项向量是否对于大语言模型能力至关重要，或将固定token身份的共享Transformer用于语言模型训练是否可行。实验显示固定编码也能获得相当的能力，独立词汇项向量不是必需的，固定身份接口为下游研究提供了控制环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨输入参数化对于大语言模型能力的重要性，以及固定token身份是否能获得类似的能力。

Method: 研究人员训练了三个使用不同输入接口的自编码语言模型，并比较其表现。

Result: 固定编码在多个评估上获得了相当的能力，虽然学习的输入模型在某些评估中表现更好，但固定接口的模型移除了大量可训练参数，证实了独立可调整的输入向量不是必需的。

Conclusion: 研究结论是固定身份接口是可行的，独立可调整的输入向量并非必需，固定身份接口也为下游研究提供了更好的控制环境。

Abstract: A trainable input embedding table assigns each vocabulary item an independently adjustable vector. We investigate whether this token-specific parameterization is required for substantial language-modeling capability, or whether a shared Transformer can learn from fixed token identities. We compare three decoder-only language models trained from scratch with the same tokenizer, contextual backbone, untied output-head architecture, and training recipe, with a target budget of 100 billion prediction tokens per model. Their input interfaces are a learned table, canonical 16-bit token-ID codes, and one fixed invertible recoding over GF(2). The fixed codes are repeated to model width without an additional trainable input projection. Both fixed-code models acquire substantial capabilities: canonical codes achieve 52.40\% HellaSwag normalized accuracy, 70.51\% PIQA accuracy, and 42.75\% LAMBADA accuracy. The learned-input control performs better on several evaluations, including HellaSwag and LAMBADA, so these results establish viability rather than performance parity. The fixed interfaces remove 100.7 million trainable parameters, yielding 1.711B-parameter models, but parameter reduction is not the central result.
