Abstract: Counter-storytelling is a powerful mechanism people use to challenge dominant narratives. Unlike other forms of counterspeech that have been widely studied in computational social science, counter-storytelling has largely been overlooked. Counter-stories are difficult to detect automatically; they are relational (defined with respect to expressions of racial stereotypes) and structurally diverse (drawing on stories that describe lived experiences, witnessed events, exemplars, and hypotheticals). We introduce a first framework for detecting and characterizing counter-storytelling against racial stereotypes at scale. This includes (1) a three-dimensional taxonomy grounded in narratology and Critical Race Theory and (2) a multi-stage pipeline that identifies relational pairs of stereotypes and counter-stories in noisy Reddit discourse. Using this pipeline, we annotate 25,549 Reddit posts across 615 communities and identify 1,312 counter-stories. Our analysis shows that speaker identity and post context shape how counter-stories are told. For example, in-group writers favor first-person testimony, often adopting the role of self-reflective insiders. Our work shows how computational methods can scale qualitative approaches to identify and characterize counter-storytelling as a contextual narrative practice, with implications for content moderation, narratology, and racial discourse analysis.

</details>


### [106] [Viva La Vida: Verification and Accumulation Failures in Multi-Agent Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04829)
*Benji Xu,Ken Zheng,Noah Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了一个无需证明助手的系统，发现该系统存在三个主要失败模式：需要一致性通过导致错误判断；单一验证器可能导致错误解决方案宣告；评审中的有用异见未被保留。研究指出，在没有外部验证的情况下，监督本身是信任边界的必需部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨语言模型在解决开放问题时作为最终证明者的局限性，以提高系统的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 该研究通过实证研究方法对一个全端到端的系统进行了详细观察和分析，记录了51,754次观测结果，在三个完整运行周期中共花费了186小时。

Result: 该研究揭示了三个关键问题：三模型验证者需要一致性投票，导致部分验证事件因语言模型输出错误或API问题而失败；单一验证器设计可能导致错误解决方案的宣告；在所有结论被否定的情况下，提取的引理忽略了否定的上下文。

Conclusion: 该研究结论表明，在没有外部验证的情况下，监督自身是确保结果准确性的关键边界，系统必须区分选择性放弃与拒绝，并确保有用信息的完整性。

Abstract: When an agentic prover works on an open problem, there is no proof assistant to fall back on: its verifier and lemma library are ultimately language models judging model outputs. We instrumented such a system end to end and analyzed $51{,}754$ traced observations across three full runs ($186$ hours, \$$5{,}694$). We find three connected failure modes. First, the three-model verifier requires unanimity and treats parse or API failure as non-approval; in $10$ of $12$ verification events, one member returned no parseable output or an API error, making acceptance arithmetically impossible without surfacing an error. Second, when the ensemble did function, one verifier approved $3$ attempts that GPT rejected, each claiming to resolve the open problem; a single-verifier design would therefore have announced a solution three times. Third, because nothing could be approved, every review was a refutation, yet the lemma extractor mines reviews as well as proofs: $24$ of $93$ lemmas ($26\%$) were extracted from rejected arguments with their refutational context removed. Taken together, these findings show that without external verification, supervision is itself a critical trust boundary: systems must distinguish abstention from rejection, preserve useful disagreement, and preserve the provenance and polarity of information before it becomes future context.

</details>


### [107] [Cluster Validation Indices as Self-Supervised Objectives for Text Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04830)
*Kishor Kumar Bhaumik,Nicolas Roque dos Santos,Neil Shah,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SilK 方法通过使用聚类验证指标作为内部簇质量度量，无需使用标签、无负样本对和多视图数据，仅需集群中心进行比较，从而在比现有最快基线快1.46倍的速度和减少45.4%的峰值GPU内存的情况下，保持了竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自监督微调方法要么需要多视图数据和内部负样本对（如对比学习方法），要么需要辅助图/网络（如无负样本方法），作者旨在提出一种新的方法，无需依赖这些额外数据，降低成本并提高效率。

Method: SilK 方法首先对语料进行聚类，然后通过回归简化轮廓系数向目标值。在整个过程中，仅将每个文档与k个集群中心进行对比，无需数据增强或负样本对。

Result: 在 BERT-base 模型上，与我们评估的最快基线相比，SilK 的每轮训练速度快了 1.46 倍，且使用的峰值 GPU 内存减少了 45.4%。在冻结编码器的线性探针下，SilK 在三个下游任务中保持与最佳基线的竞争力。

Conclusion: SilK 方法通过内部聚类验证指标革新了自监督微调过程，不仅提高了效率，还减少了资源消耗，并且在实际应用中表现稳定。

Abstract: Self-supervised fine-tuning refines the embedding space of a pretrained language encoder without labels. However, the commonly used approaches are computationally expensive. Specifically, contrastive learning-based methods need multiview data and in-batch negative examples, while negative-free approaches require auxiliary graphs/networks. An interesting question arises: can self-supervised fine-tuning be done without relying on either additional negatives or graph data? To answer this question, we introduce SilK (Silhouette-guided K-means), which trains on a Cluster Validation Index, an internal measure of cluster quality without using labels. SilK clusters the corpus and then regresses a simplified silhouette toward a target value. Each document is compared only against the k cluster centroids, never against other documents, so the method needs no augmentation, no negative pairs and one view per document. On BERT-base, SilK trains 1.46x faster per epoch than the fastest baseline we evaluate and uses 45.4% less peak GPU memory than the leanest one. Under frozen-encoder linear probing, SilK stays competitive with the best baselines on three downstream tasks.

</details>


### [108] [No Hindsight for LLM Fact-Checkers: Measuring Leakage Channels in Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04888)
*Kuan-Hua Wu Lu,Yohanes Andre Setiawan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析规模化的自动事实核查过程，发现标准基准数据集在评估大规模语言模型时可能存在不准确，尤其是在处理不可知信息时。研究通过重建证据时间点并检测模型中的参数泄漏，发现延迟信息外泄比基于互信息训练惩罚更为常见，允许发布后的证据提高了零样本准确度，但这一效果在依赖检索的基准数据集上不大明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示标准基准数据集在评估大规模语言模型事实核查性能时可能存在误导性问题。

Method: 研究使用了AVeriTeC和QuanTemp++数据集，通过重建证据时间点并检测模型中的参数泄漏来分析。

Result: 研究发现，未来信息的参数泄漏在聚合准确度下的表现较为隐蔽，使用简单的表征瓶颈可以更有效地减少这种泄漏。允许发布后的证据在AVeriTeC上提高了零样本准确度6.3个百分点，而在依赖检索的QuanTemp++上影响不大。

Conclusion: 该研究结论指出，如果没有考虑到在声明时间点可以获取的信息，那么基于误导性基准数据集获得的事实核查性能可能被高估。

Abstract: As automated fact-checking scales on social media, large language model (LLM) verdict scores can look stronger than warranted. One reason is that evaluations mix in information that was not knowable at claim time. Two channels are easy to conflate: outcomes memorized in pre-training and retrieved evidence published after the claim. Yet standard benchmarks rarely separate the two. In this study we measure both channels on AVeriTeC and QuanTemp++ by reconstructing point-in-time evidence conditions and probing for outcome information encoded in model representations. We find substantial evidence of parametric leakage, that can be hidden by the aggregate accuracy, while a simple representation bottleneck reduces this future leakage more efficiently than a mutual-information-based training penalty. We also find that allowing post-claim evidence inflates zero-shot accuracy by 6.3 points in AVeriTeC while the effect is negligible in QuanTemp++, where retrieval provides little post-claim evidence. These results show that misinformation benchmarks can overstate fact-checking performance when they do not account for what information was actually available at claim time.

</details>


### [109] [A Systematic Analysis of the Predictive Power of LM Surprisal in Reading Chinese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04898)
*Hongao Zhu,Muxiaoqiao Xu,Yikang Liu,Siyuan Song,Yuxia Wang,Byung-Doh Oh,Hai Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了LM衍生的令牌级惊愕值对普通话阅读时间的预测能力，提出了一种名为SMS的对齐方案，并使用不同大小的中国-Pythia模型，在不同的语料库中，发现惊愕值对阅读时间具有预测能力，但这种能力是否随模型大小和训练量的增加而增加在不同语料库中表现不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索LM衍生的令牌级惊愕值对普通话阅读时间的具体影响，并通过不同大小的模型和语料库分析这一因素的变化。

Method: 研究方法包括提出SMS对齐方案，并使用不同大小的Pythia模型对普通话语料库进行测试，以分析惊愕值对阅读时间的预测能力。

Result: 研究结果表明，惊愕值对普通话阅读时间具有预测性，但模型大小和训练量的影响因语料库而异。在某些情况下，模型增大提高了预测效果，而在其他情况下则表现为相反的规模效应。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，惊愕值对普通话阅读时间的预测性具有语料库特异性，因此在得出关于这种预测性的普遍性结论时需要小心。

Abstract: This study analyzes the predictive power of LM-derived, token-level surprisal on Mandarin Chinese reading times. We first propose the Shortest Matching Sequence (SMS), an alignment scheme that maps between the word segmentation assumed by eye-tracking corpora and the LMs' subword tokenization, as the two tokenizations often disagree in the context of Mandarin Chinese. Then, using a suite of Chinese-Pythia models (14M-1.4B) trained on scratch with 30B tokens, we examine how well surprisal predicts first fixation duration, gaze duration, and total reading time in three paragraph-level eye-tracking corpora of Mandarin Chinese (GECO-CN, HKP, and MECO). Contrary to previous null findings, our results show that surprisal is predictive of Chinese reading times. However, whether predictive power scales with model size and the amount of training is corpus-specific: bigger models predict better in GECO-CN, whereas inverse scaling emerges in HKP and, at the largest sizes, in MECO. Subsequently, we tested one possible explanation for the inverse scaling in HKP and found that checkpoints whose surprisal remains closer to $n$-gram statistics are better predictors of reading. All in all, the predictive power of surprisal on Chinese reading time measurements is corpus-specific, which cautions against drawing scaling conclusions from a single corpus.

</details>


### [110] [Rewrite What Matters: Adaptive Multilingual Query Rewriting for Reasoning via Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04899)
*Rui Qi,Yufeng Chen,Yunlong Liang,Chuan Meng,Sijin Lu,Ge Shi,Jinan Xu,Fandong Meng,Kaiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的多语言查询重写框架mRewriter-R1，它将多语言查询重写视为多轮顺序决策过程，并通过适应性操作符选择实现多方面优化，实验表明该框架在不同推理模型上均优于其他强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的多语言查询重写方法常常使用单一策略（如翻译），未能充分考虑不同场景下所需的多样化语义变换。因此，本文寻求通过一个基于强化学习的框架mRewriter-R1来解决这一问题。

Method: mRewriter-R1将多轮多语言查询重写建模为多轮顺序决策过程，模型通过可调节的操作符选择进行适配化优化。

Result: 该方法在不同的大型推理模型上表现优于其他强基线。此外，学习到的策略可以根据查询特征自适应选择重写操作符，跨不同推理任务具有较强的泛化能力，并且具备与不同推理语言模型无缝集成的能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法能够有效应对跨语言查询导致的不同推理路径和性能差距问题，展示了其在多语言环境下的优越性和灵活性。

Abstract: In multilingual scenarios, queries with equivalent semantics but in different languages could guide the model into different reasoning trajectories, leading to performance disparities. To mitigate this gap, previous studies typically apply a one-size-fits-all query rewriting strategy, such as translation, which overlooks the fact that different scenarios require diverse types of semantic transformations. In this paper, we propose mRewriter-R1, an agentic multilingual query rewriting framework with reinforcement learning. Unlike single-turn rewriting, mRewriter-R1 formulates multilingual query rewriting as a multi-turn sequential decision-making process, where the model dynamically performs multi-aspect optimization through adaptive operator selection. Experimental results demonstrate that mRewriter-R1 outperforms all strong multilingual rewriting baselines on different large reasoning backbones. Further analyses show that the learned policy can adaptively decide on rewriting operators according to query characteristics, exhibiting strong generalization ability across diverse reasoning tasks, and plug-and-play compatibility with heterogeneous reasoning language models.

</details>


### [111] [Scaling Verifiable Environments for Long-horizon Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04906)
*Jiazheng Zhang,Long Ma,Yunxian Yang,Zhiheng Xi,Zhikai Lei,Yajie Yang,Chenyang Liao,Enyu Zhou,Yang Nan,Yuchen Tian,Senjie Jin,Yibo Wang,Wei He,Boyang Liu,Jixuan Huang,Xin Guo,Zhezheng Hao,Xinbing Liang,Zhihao Zhang,Changzhi Zhou,Wiggin Zhou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Clarenceai,Aiden Adams*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WorkForge 是一种可扩展的合成框架，可以构建由真实资源支持的可供验证的工作者代理环境。通过识别所需的资源、决策和交付成果，组织相关真实文件，并从中提取可以验证的实证依据来生成详细的任务说明和验证者，使得训练后的模型在多个领域的表现显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的训练环境存在工程开销高和环境规模受限的问题，而合成方法则会牺牲工作空间的复杂性、真实感或实地验证可靠性。为解决这一问题，我们引入了 WorkForge 框架。

Method: WorkForge 从专家工作流开始，首先识别每个工作流所需的信息、决策和交付成果，然后检索相关的真实文件，组织到一个工作空间中。通过检查工作空间，提取关于内容的具体可验证事实，这些事实锚点确定了工作空间可以支持的任务类型及其结果验证方法。WorkForge 能够从中直接推导出每个任务的说明、解决方案计划，以及配套的程序和语义验证器，并确保验证可以追溯到实际的环境证据。

Result: 通过 WorkForge 构建了跨越40个专业领域的16700个可验证环境，并且使用 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B-Base 训练后， GDPVal 指标从 45.5 提高到 73.6，APEX Score 指标从 5.0 提高到 21.3，Qwen3.5-27B 也超越了强大对手，展现出强大的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，拟议方法的有效性，并且表现出一致的扩展行为，不论是数据量增加还是交互时间轴增加的情况下。

Abstract: Work agents operate over digital artifacts to execute professional knowledge-intensive work, requiring training environments that support long-horizon interaction and trustworthy verification. However, hand-crafted environments incur prohibitive engineering overhead that prevents environment scaling, whereas synthesis methods sacrifice workspace complexity, realism, or grounded verifiability. To bridge this gap, we introduce WorkForge, a scalable synthesis framework for constructing verifiable work-agent environments from real-world resources. Starting from expert workflows, WorkForge first identifies the resources, decisions, and deliverables required by each workflow. It then retrieves relevant real-world files and organizes them into a workspace. WorkForge inspects the workspace to extract concrete, checkable facts about its content. These factual anchors fix which task types the workspace can support and how their outcomes can be verified. Therefore, WorkForge derives each task's instructions, solution plan, and complementary programmatic and semantic verifiers directly from these factual anchors, keeping verification traceable to observable workspace evidence. Furthermore, we construct 16.7K verifiable environments across 40 professional domains, with workspaces collectively covering 60 file types. Post-training Qwen3.5-35B-A3B-Base improves GDPVal from 45.5 to 73.6 and APEX Score from 5.0 to 21.3, while enabling Qwen3.5-27B to achieve highly competitive performance and outperform strong competitors. Our analyses confirm the efficacy of the proposed method and reveal consistent scaling behaviors across both data volume and interaction horizons.

</details>


### [112] [From Overloaded to Guaranteed: High-Throughput Multi-SLO Enforcement for LoRA-Assisted On-Premise LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04956)
*Zeshen Zhang,Han Zhao,Weihao Cui,Quan Chen,Yu Liu,Yongjun Deng,Jing Yang,Jiuchen Shi,Chen Chen,Youmin Chen,Yu Feng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HALO的调度方法，针对本地部署的带有LoRA辅助的大语言模型，通过空间复用策略和SLO感知调度器提高了服务质量并提升了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的服务框架由于LoRA层的计算开销和批次调度的僵化性，在资源受限的服务器上同时为多个LoRA适配的服务提供异构的服务级别目标（SLO）时，会严重违反SLO。因此，需要一种新的调度方法来解决这一问题。

Method: HALO方法包含两个创新点：空间复用策略通过分割GPU Streaming Multiprocessors (SM)来重叠Base和LoRA计算；SLO感知调度器根据请求级别的空闲时间解耦请求执行。这种方法优先处理紧急任务并利用空闲预算来优化流量。

Result: 我们的评估表明，与最先进的基线相比，HALO在减少SLO违反的同时，提高了吞吐量。

Conclusion: HALO方法通过这两种创新策略有效地解决了LoRA辅助的大语言模型本地部署中的服务质量问题。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become essential in privacy-sensitive sectors like hospitals and government agencies, the on-premise LLM servers offer a cost-effective and secure alternative to public cloud services. However, these resource-constrained servers struggle to guarantee heterogeneous Service Level Objectives (SLOs) when serving multiple LoRA-adapted services simultaneously. Existing serving frameworks suffer from severe SLO violations due to the computational overhead of LoRA layers and the rigid nature of batch scheduling. To address this, we propose HALO, a scheduling method tailored for LoRA-assisted on-premise LLM deployment. HALO introduces two key innovations: a spatial multiplexing strategy that overlaps Base and LoRA computations by partitioning GPU Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs), and an SLO-aware scheduler that decouples request execution based on "request-level slack." By prioritizing urgent tasks and utilizing idle budget for traffic shaping, HALO significantly mitigates resource contention. Our evaluation demonstrates that HALO minimizes SLO violations while improving throughput compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [113] [Building LLM Agent Systems the Deep Learning Way: From Modular Design to Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04961)
*Tao Feng,Pengrui Han,Zhongjie Dai,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过模块化构建大型语言模型（LLM）代理系统，借鉴深度学习的成功模块如MLPs、注意力机制等，设计前向推理和反馈机制，并利用类似于神经架构搜索(NAS)的方法进行优化。实验表明，这种方法在多个方面显著提升了代理系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于其他领域的见解或启发式方法手动构建LLM代理系统，需要大量的手工程设计且无法完全优化下游任务。借鉴深度学习的成功经验，提出了一种模块化构建LLM代理系统的方案。

Method: 基于深度学习的模块化构建方法，包括LLM构建块（如检索、记忆、提示策略）与深度学习模块（如MLPs、注意力机制等）的类比；设计前向推理和反馈机制，将提示视为深层模型的权重；利用类似于NAS的搜索算法进行模块配置搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明，这种方法在多个方面显著提升代理系统的性能，具体包括：采用类似深度学习架构组织LLM模块，性能提升显著；自动提示优化使反馈纳入任务，提升至少5%；NAS等算法进一步优化代理系统架构，性能提升11%。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了通过类似深度学习的方法构建LLM代理系统的潜力和机遇。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI research and enabled exciting agent systems. To build a complex LLM agent system, most existing research relies on insights from other domains or heuristics to manually build the agent system. However, this approach often requires heavy hand-engineering and fails to fully optimize for the downstream task of interest. Inspired by the tremendous success of deep learning, we propose to construct LLM agent systems in a modular manner, similar to building a deep neural network. Our key insight is to make analogies between LLM building blocks, such as retrievals, memories, and prompting strategies, and the successful deep learning modules, such as MLPs, attention, and recurrent modules. We further design forward inference and feedback mechanisms for LLMs, where prompts in LLMs are considered as the weights in deep models, and the prompt optimization from feedback is analogous to the back-propagation algorithm. We additionally leverage a search algorithm to search for the best configuration of LLM agent systems, similar to the neural architecture search (NAS) in deep learning research. Comprehensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed deep learning recipe for LLM agent systems is highly effective, in particular: (1) Organizing LLM modules into deep-learning-style architectures yields noticeable performance gain; (2) Automatic prompt optimization, equivalent to backpropagation, is efficient in incorporating feedback from the task of interest and achieves at least 5% performance improvement; (3) NAS equivalent algorithm works well for further optimizing the LLM agent system architecture with 11% performance gain compared with randomly designed architectures. Overall, our research demonstrates the exciting opportunity of transferring the success of deep learning to building LLM agent systems.

</details>


### [114] [TrajLong: Co-Designing Agentic and Long-Context Supervision for Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04973)
*Miao Peng,Qintong Zhang,Nuo Chen,Yuhan Li,Guochen Yan,Xinran Gu,Hongqiu Wu,Hai Wang,Lydell Huang,Wentao Zhang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究引入TrajLong框架，将代理轨迹编译成长语境训练任务，提供密集监督，针对证据接地、跨证据聚合及时间状态维护三种原子能力进行中间训练和监督微调，实验表明该方法提升了多种长语境和代理基准的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理在处理编码、搜索和工作场所任务时依赖于长时间段的数据来聚合并推理长期交互历史，但如何组织这些长轨迹中的信息以提供有效的中间训练监督仍然没有得到充分探索。

Method: 研究设计并实现了一个名为TrajLong的新框架，通过将代理轨迹转化为长语境训练任务，并提供密集监督来促进代理能力的发展。研究选取证据接地、跨证据聚合及时间状态维护三种代表性原子能力作为实验对象。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用TrajLong框架编译的数据进行中间训练后，代理模型在多种长语境和代理基准测试的成绩均有所提高；通过消融实验，发现比基线模型有改进。

Conclusion: 研究强调了长期推理能力和代理执行能力之间的共性需求，为设计中间训练数据提供了一种有原则的方法，能够促进下游代理能力的发展。

Abstract: LLM agents for coding, search, and workplace tasks increasingly rely on long-context capabilities to effectively aggregate and reason over extended interaction histories. Recent work has incorporated agent trajectories into mid-training stage, drawing on their naturally long and interaction-rich structure. Yet how to organize the information within these trajectories into effective mid-training supervision remains underexplored. In this work, we investigate the relationship between long-context and agent atomic capabilities and introduce TrajLong, a novel framework that compiles trajectories into long-context training tasks with dense supervision, targeting three representative atomic capabilities: evidence grounding, cross-evidence aggregation, and temporal state maintenance. We mid-train Qwen3-14B-Base and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base with data compiled by TrajLong, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Experiments on 6 long-context and 12 agent benchmarks demonstrate broad performance gains, with controlled ablations showing improvements over raw and masked trajectory baselines. Capability-level analyses further reveal task-dependent associations between long-context and agent atomic capabilities. These findings suggest that the shared capability demands of long-context reasoning and agent execution provide a principled basis for designing mid-training data to develop downstream agent capabilities.

</details>


### [115] [IREA: Intermediate Representation-based Embedding Alignment for Normative RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04974)
*Mirae Han,Sihyeong Yeom,Harksoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为规范RAG的方法，通过引入中间表示嵌入对齐（IREA）算法，将语境丰富的叙述性查询和概括性的规范性陈述映射到共享的情境-行为表示，以改善规范检索和下游伦理判断。实验结果表明，这种方法提高了多个场景下的规范检索和伦理判断效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于伦理规范的多样性和相对性，传统的语言模型难以完全内化这些规范。因此，研究提出了规范RAG方法，通过引入IREA算法，弥补这一不足。

Method: 研究设计了IREA算法，这是一种双向对齐方法，可以将不同类型的文本映射到共享的情境-行为表示中，从而在嵌入空间中减少表面差异并提高对齐效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，与现有方法相比，规范RAG方法在多个设置下的规范检索和伦理判断效果得到了显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了双向对齐方法在规范RAG中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance across various tasks, but they still struggle with questions involving ethical judgment. Previous studies have attempted to train LLMs on ethical standards, but the diversity and relativity of ethical norms make them difficult to fully internalize in model parameters. As an alternative, we introduce normative RAG, a retrieval-augmented approach that supports ethical judgment using external normative knowledge. Normative retrieval involves a distinct asymmetry between context rich narrative queries and generalized normative statements. Existing factual retrieval methods rely on query-only expansion into a document-like form, making them insufficient for resolving this asymmetry. Therefore, we propose Intermediate Representation-based Embedding Alignment (IREA), a bidirectional alignment method that maps both text types into a shared situation-behavior representation. This representation captures ethically salient contextual and behavioral information in a normalized form, reducing surface-level discrepancies and improving alignment in the embedding space. Experimental results show that IREA improves normative retrieval and downstream ethical judgment across multiple settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of bidirectional alignment for normative RAG.

</details>


### [116] [When LLMs Sit Above Diagnostic Tools: Unrealized Complementarity in Industrial Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05031)
*Donghwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大型语言模型（LLM）与外部诊断信息的整合效果，在五个数据集中没有观察到LLM整合外部信息的一致优势。即使LLM结合了专家信息，在某些情况下仍然落后于单独的专家判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解大型语言模型在实际应用中的表现及其在与专业工具结合时的效果。

Method: 研究者通过五个不同的诊断数据集（轴承振动、过程监控、半导体设备等）评估LLM整合外部诊断信息的效果，采用对照试验的方式，通过独立的LLM版本与整合版LLM的比较，以及和单独专家判断的对比，分析了实际应用环境中的表现。

Result: 在五个数据集中，LLM整合外部信息并未表现出一致的优势。直接整合对比显示，四组数据集中均未出现LLM整合版本比单独专家版本更有优势的情况。在固定协议的Tennessee东曼确认集中，未受外部信息帮助的准确率为64.67%，整合LLM与专家信息的准确率为77.43%，而单独专家的准确率为83.33%。专家信息显著提高了LLM的准确性（提高了12.8个点），但集成输出依然比专家的准确率低约5.9个点。双源选择器的性能达到92.76%，表明集成输出未能充分利用专家信息的互补性。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，在评估整合系统时，应单独评估两个源头的质量，即综合层不应仅与未经外部信息帮助的LLM进行对比，而应该与更强的独立组件进行比较。此外，专家信息的补充并没有显著减少基于假设的推理努力，亦未解决整合问题。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as integration layers above specialized tools, but a stronger component does not necessarily produce a stronger combined system. Across five diagnostic datasets (bearing vibration, process monitoring, semiconductor equipment), we study whether an LLM can reliably use external diagnostic information; paired repeat calls separate advice effects from output instability. In all five, conflicting external information overturned initially correct LLM judgments. Among the four datasets with direct integration comparisons, none showed a consistent advantage for implicit LLM integration over the stronger standalone source. On a Tennessee Eastman confirmation set whose protocol was fixed before evaluation, unaided accuracy was 64.67%, implicit LLM-specialist integration 77.43%, and the specialist alone 83.33%. Specialist information improved the LLM by 12.8 points (95% interval 9.7 to 15.9), yet the integrated output stayed 5.9 points below the specialist (95% interval -12.0 to -0.7). A two-source selector oracle reached 92.76%, indicating complementarity that the integrated output did not fully realize. The integrated output missed 140 of 295 specialist corrections (47.5%) but lost 15 of 99 initially correct LLM judgments (15.2%). The deficit remained under prompt and specialist sensitivity analyses. Among CWRU cases solved under both evidence presentations, task-aligned physical evidence yielded lower estimates of susceptibility to incorrect advice in six of seven models (five intervals excluding zero); higher reasoning effort gave no reliable reduction in five models, and a separate four-model TEP analysis gave no clear evidence that it resolves the integration problem. Source quality and integration quality should be evaluated separately: an integration layer should be compared with its stronger standalone component, not only with the unaided LLM.

</details>


### [117] [Causal Improvement Graph for Agentic Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05039)
*Junjie Zhang,Shunyu Liu,Haoyu Wang,Ting-En Lin,Yongbin Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果改进图（CIG），这是一种元运行时框架，通过图形治理将改进状态外部化，允许先前发现直接指导后续运行时优化。CIG 在不同代理任务中发现了比以前的元运行时基线更强的运行时，并且对任务求解器和提案者的选择具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元运行时方法主要通过语言模型（LLM）为基础的提案者基于积累的实验发现确定后续的运行时修订，这使得维护进化改进状态的任务负担增加。因此，提出CIG以外部化改进状态，使其更加持久和可操作。

Method: CIG通过构建因果图中的证据、假设、干预和结果节点，代表观察到的内容、解释、测试解释的方法以及评估的结果。这些节点的结构关系保留了改进状态如何随迭代变化。

Result: 在各种代理任务中，CIG发现比以前的元运行时基线更强的运行时，并且对任务求解器和提案者的选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CIG的结构消融进一步支持了改进状态设计为显式，并通过图形治理实现进化。

Abstract: Agentic Harness is the runtime that constructs task context and controls execution flow, thereby shaping overall agent performance. Given a fixed model and external evaluation, automated Harness optimization seeks to improve this runtime through an iterative proposal--evaluation loop to better solve target tasks. Existing meta-harness methods mainly adopt proposer-centric discovery, in which an LLM-based proposer integrates accumulated experimental findings to determine subsequent Harness revisions. This places the burden of maintaining the evolving improvement state on the proposer as history expands and its underlying experimental logic becomes harder to discern. In this paper, we introduce the Causal Improvement Graph (CIG), a graph-governed meta-harness framework that externalizes the evolving improvement state in a persistent graph, allowing prior findings to directly govern subsequent Harness optimization through local proposer operations. CIG grows and links Evidence, Hypothesis, Intervention, and Outcome nodes to represent what was observed, how it may be explained, how to test that explanation, and what the evaluation reveals. Their structural relations preserve how the improvement state changes across iterations, allowing local proposers to build directly on relations among prior findings rather than recover them from raw history. Across various agent tasks, CIG discovers stronger Harnesses than previous meta-harness baselines and remains robust to the choice of task solver and proposer. Structural ablations further support the design of an explicit improvement state with graph-governed evolution.

</details>


### [118] [AraYoungVoices: A Diverse L1/L2 Corpus of Arabic Child and Adolescent Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05044)
*Shammur Absar Chowdhury,Zien Sheikh Ali,Houssam Eddine-Othman Lachemat,Hamdy Mubarak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了AraYoungVoices，这是一个包含阿拉伯儿童和青少年读音的151.72小时语料库。研究发现，第二语言（L2）发音比第一语言（L1）发音更具挑战性，并且年龄特异性微调可以提高同龄群体的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ASR系统主要针对成人的自然语言，但对于儿童、青少年和二语使用者存在较大的性能差距，因此该研究旨在构建一个跨年龄和语言背景的阿拉伯读音语料库以改善这些群体的ASR表现。

Method: 构建了一个包含286名年龄7至18岁，146名阿拉伯母语、140名二语使用者的语料库，分别针对预训练ASR模型进行零样本和微调测试，评估了在不同评估场景下的表现。

Result: 研究结果显示L2发音比L1发音更具挑战性，其中年轻L2发音者的错误率最高。年龄特异性微调主要提升了对应年龄组的表现，而联合微调则在各群体间提供了更强的平衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，有针对性的年龄微调可以提高特定年龄组的ASR性能，而联合微调则提供了更广泛的群体支持。

Abstract: State-of-the-art ASR systems primarily target native adult speech, leading to substantial performance gaps for children, adolescents, and L2 speakers. We introduce AraYoungVoices, a 151.72-hour Arabic read-speech corpus from 286 speakers aged 7--18, comprising AraKids (7--12) and AraTeens (13--18). The corpus includes 146 native Arabic (L1) and 140 second-language (L2) speakers, with native speakers spanning Egyptian, Gulf, Levantine, and North African dialectal backgrounds and L2 speakers representing diverse linguistic backgrounds across the Americas, Asia, Africa, and Europe. We benchmark four pretrained ASR models under zero-shot and fine-tuned settings using unseen-speaker-$\&$-unseen-prompt (USUP) and unseen-speaker-$\&$-seen-prompt (USSP) evaluations. Results show that L2 speech remains substantially more challenging than L1 speech, with the largest errors observed mainly for younger L2 speakers. Age-specific fine-tuning improves the matched age group, while joint fine-tuning provides a stronger balance across populations. ASR hypotheses are also consistently closer to the standard reading prompt than to the verbatim transcription, particularly for L2 speech, suggesting partial normalization of reading deviations.

</details>


### [119] [Usage-Modulated Sentiment Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05069)
*Hongfei Du,Jiacheng Shi,Yanfu Zhang,Gang Zhou,Ye Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管共享情感方向在多个模型中表现稳健，移除该方向后，模型仍能保留83.3%-90.9%的情感极性差异，并且在删除了情感方向后，模型保留了88.9%-97.5%的情感表达差异。残留结构中存在紧凑可重复的使用条件结构。有针对性的删除增强了未见过的使用条件度量，而沿残留情感成分生成的输出在目标情感比较中82.8%被偏好，同时保持了98.7%的情感极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更全面地理解情感表达，研究探讨了情感方向之外模型中潜在的情感表达结构，发现除了共享的情感向量，还存在由语调和受众适应等因素构建的情感结构。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个控制对的语料库，保留事件内容不变，仅改变情感极性和使用因素，分析了Llama、Mistral和Gemma模型，并通过擦除和生成时间语调引导测试残余结构。

Result: 研究发现，共享的情感方向在多个模型中表现稳健，但移除后仍保留了大部分情感极性差异。残留结构中存在紧凑可重复的使用条件结构。有针对性的删除增强未见过的使用条件度量，沿残留情感成分生成的输出在目标情感比较中82.8%被偏好，同时保持98.7%的情感极性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，除了共享的情感方向，模型中还存在由语调和使用因素构建的可重复的情感结构。这为更深入地理解情感表达提供了新视角。

Abstract: Prior work suggests that sentiment can often be captured by approximately linear directions in LLM activation spaces, but a single direction may not fully capture sentiment representations. In natural communication, sentiment is shaped not only by polarity but also by usage factors, such as tone and audience adaptation. We test whether these factors systematically modulate sentiment representations beyond a shared sentiment direction. We construct a controlled paired dataset that holds event content fixed while varying sentiment polarity and usage factors, and analyze Llama, Mistral, and Gemma. We identify a shared sentiment direction, remove it, and test the residual structure through erasure and generation-time tone steering. Across models, the shared direction is robust (median cosine 0.953-0.975), yet removing it leaves 0.833-0.909 of the original positive-negative representation-difference norm. The residuals contain compact, reproducible usage-conditioned structure. Targeted erasure weakens held-out usage metrics more than random and label-shuffled controls. On Llama, outputs steered along residualized tone components are preferred in 92.8% of blind target-tone comparisons while preserving the requested sentiment polarity in 98.7% of evaluated outputs.

</details>


### [120] [Self-Generated Feedback Destabilizes Test-Time Training: A Causal Decomposition of Long-Horizon Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05076)
*Cheng Luo,Bing Li,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在测试时训练模型存储信息会恶化预测独立人类撰写的文本，尤其是在TTT-E2E模型配置的125M、760M和3B中。通过固定生成和记录重放，可以减少损害。循环适应后的更新成本增加，而结算机制可以在保留对真实文本的适应性的同时，避免可能的预测错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索测试时训练过程中模型更新机制对模型性能的影响，特别是当模型在其自身的输出上进行学习时，从而寻求更加稳定和准确的模型。

Method: 研究通过不同模型配置和实验方法，包括固定生成、记录重放、闭合循环适应和结算机制等多种策略，对比了更新和不更新模型权重对预测表现的影响。

Result: 结果表明，在测试时训练过程中，保留生成文本的更新会破坏对独立人类撰写的文本的预测性能。固定生成和记录重放可以在一定程度上减少这种损害。在闭合循环适应后，单个更新会导致局部冲突，表现为更新更好地预测了其来源，但新真实文本更差。而采用结算机制可以在保留真实文本适应性的同时避免预测错误。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，在测试时训练模型时，应考虑在更新前检查独立证据的预测，以防止可能的预测错误，即在更新模型之前评估其在独立数据集上的表现。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) lets a model store information in its weights during inference. When the model learns from its own output, however, each update also changes the model that generates the next training example. Across 128K-token streams, retaining generated-text updates worsens prediction on independent human-written text with three TTT-E2E model configurations (labeled 125M, 760M, and 3B). The same failure occurs when Adam updates Qwen3-4B's existing weights. The same update mechanisms can improve on real text, so writing itself is not the failure. Three matched comparisons trace the causal pathway. Fixed Generation removes over 98% of the damage at 125M and 760M by using a frozen model to generate training chunks. Recorded Replay separates the loss caused by reading degraded text from the additional loss stored by updating on it. A paired one-update comparison then shows the local conflict: an update predicts its source better but new real text worse. This cost grows after Closed Loop adaptation, with a few trajectories accounting for most large failures. Finally, Settlement evaluates the candidate state on independent real text before commitment. It leaves mean endpoint gaps of 0.07 and -0.02 nats at 125M and 760M while retaining real-text adaptation. These results motivate checking prediction on independent evidence before retaining an update.

</details>


### [121] [Towards cross-cultural study of folksong lyrics with machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05084)
*Anna Dvořáková,Anna Aljanaki,Danbinaerin Han,Peter van Kranenburg,Matěj Kratochvíl,Inna Lisniak,Zdeněk Vejvoda,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用NLP技术将五种语言的民谣歌词合集机器翻译为中间语言，并通过主题模型研究歌词内容与其社会功能之间的关系。结果显示，除了印欧语系，其他语言的翻译质量较差，歌词内容与民谣的社会功能关系并不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着NLP技术的发展，研究人员能够跨越语言障碍，对不同语言的民谣歌词进行分析，揭示跨文化背景下民谣歌词的社会功能及其相互关系。

Method: 通过收集五种语言的民谣歌词合集，使用预训练的神经主题模型进行机器翻译，并采用主题模型探索歌词内容和社会功能之间的联系。

Result: 研究发现，非印欧语系的翻译质量较差，歌词内容与民谣的社会功能之间关系有限。

Conclusion: 尽管这是跨文化民谣歌词分析的初步研究，但结果表明，人们在世界各地的民间音乐中演唱的内容可能揭示出超越传统音乐学类型学的跨文化关系。

Abstract: Music is universally present in human societies. Ethnomusicologists have long been documenting the diverse expressions of human musicality, and comparative musicology has recently brought several studies of folksong to a more global scale. Such cross-cultural research has not been conducted on lyrics: the language barrier has so far prevented work with multi-lingual data. However, Natural Language Processing (NLP) technologies have reached a stage where this language barrier may no longer be prohibitive. Combining folksong lyrics corpora across five languages, we machine-translate them to a pivot language with a pre-trained neural topic model, and we examine the relationship between content and social function within each language, and across languages for wedding songs. As expected, human evaluation of translation results shows that non-Indo-European languages suffer from overall worse translation quality. Experiments with topic models then indicate that the content of lyrics is at best partially related to the social function of folksongs across all languages. These experiments are just first steps into cross-cultural folk musics lyrics analysis; however, they do indicate that a previously unobserved web of cross-cultural relationships beyond ethnomusicological typologies may be uncovered through the study of what people sing across the world's diverse folk musics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Training Numerical Intelligence via Auto-Diagnosis and Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03872)
*Peter Chen,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADSD框架通过诊断和技能发现相结合的方法，提高了多种数值求解器的性能，包括准确性、鲁棒性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理生成科学代码能力的增强，ADSD框架旨在解决仅依赖执行反馈无法揭示求解器性能差的根本原因和解决方案的问题。

Method: ADSD采用诊断优先的方法，通过诊断为何求解器表现不佳，然后利用诊断结果来指导合适的数值方法发现。框架将这些知识包装成可复用的求解器技能。

Result: 在四个挑战性的数值领域内，ADSD都能提高求解器的性能，其中GOC-500电力潮流示例中，ADSD将平均求解器误差减少了近71倍，同时这些改进还能应用到未见过的网络拓扑和操作模式中。

Conclusion: 研究结论是ADSD框架的有效性已通过多个数值领域的验证，显示出它能够系统地提高求解器性能的过程。

Abstract: AI agents are becoming increasingly capable of generating scientific code, but generating code is not the same as improving the algorithms behind it. For numerical solvers, execution feedback can expose poor performance, but rarely reveals its underlying cause and how to address it. We introduce Auto-Diagnosis and Skill Discovery (ADSD), a framework that links numerical diagnosis to reusable solver self-improvement. ADSD follows a diagnosis-first paradigm that first explains why a solver performs poorly, then uses this diagnosis to guide the discovery of appropriate numerical methods. The resulting knowledge is packaged into reusable solver skills, turning solver improvement from trial-and-error editing into a structured process of diagnosis, discovery, and implementation. Across four challenging numerical domains--power flow equation, AC optimal power flow control, stiff ordinary differential equations, and heterogeneous diffusion PDEs--ADSD consistently improves solver accuracy, robustness, and efficiency. On GOC-500 power flow, for example, ADSD reduces mean solver error by nearly $71\times$, with improvements further transferring to unseen grid topologies and operating regimes.

</details>


### [123] [REACT: Physically and Chemically Consistent Reconstruction of Marine Active Tracers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03888)
*Wenbin Dai,Hao Zheng,Shiyu Liang,Chaofan Sun,Xueying Zhang,Hanbo Huang,Xuan Gong,Yiran Zhang,Enhui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REACT框架通过解耦传输、活性修正和化学解码，使用保守的对流-扩散求解器传输潜碳酸盐状态，用源模块捕获非保守的碳循环变化，将修正状态解码为pH，并通过碳酸盐平衡约束输出，从而保持pH为目标同时确保基础碳状态的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋酸化监控和海洋碳循环理解需要从稀疏观测中重建全球海面pH值。传统方法如传统同化和逆模型虽然物理基础牢固但成本高昂，而新的黑盒和物理引导AI模型虽然效率提高，但主要针对被动追踪器，未考虑pH作为活跃碳酸盐跟踪器的特性。

Method: REACT框架包含保守对流-扩散求解器用于传输潜碳酸盐状态，源模块捕捉非保守碳循环变化，通过碳酸盐平衡约束输出进行化学解码，确保pH作为目标的同时维持碳状态的一致性。

Result: 在仿真数据上，REACT减少了pH的NRMSE（14.7%）和化学一致性误差（24.0%）。跨时间尺度评估显示了模型对从粗时间尺度到精细时间尺度误差累积的稳健性，通过消融研究验证了每个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: REACT框架通过创新方法有效解决了现有模型在处理活性碳酸盐追踪器时遇到的问题，展示了在监测海洋酸化和理解海洋碳循环方面的潜力。

Abstract: Reconstructing global sea surface pH from sparse observations is critical for monitoring ocean acidification and understanding marine carbon cycling. Traditional assimilation and inverse models are physically grounded but costly for large-scale reconstruction. Recent black-box and physics-guided AI models improve efficiency, but are mainly designed for passive tracers, where the reconstructed variable is also the transported inventory. In contrast, pH is an active carbonate tracer: it is the prediction target, while dissolved inorganic carbon (DIC) is the conserved carbon inventory. This mismatch can produce low pH error while violating carbonate closure and source-free carbon conservation. To address this, we introduce \textbf{REACT}, a carbon-first reconstruction framework that decouples transport, active correction, and chemical decoding. REACT transports a latent carbonate state with a conservative advection--diffusion solver, captures non-conservative carbon-cycle variations with a source module, decodes the corrected state into pH, and constrains the output through carbonate equilibrium. This design keeps pH as the target while enforcing consistency on the underlying carbon state. On simulation data, REACT reduces pH NRMSE by (14.7%) and chemical consistency error by (24.0%) over the best baseline. Cross-temporal-scale evaluations show robustness against error accumulation from coarse to fine temporal scales, and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [124] [Proxy Confidence: Auditing Black-Box LLM Agents with a Surrogate's Log-Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03894)
*Yikai Zhao,Saurabh Pandey,Pradeep Kumar Misra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种低成本的开源替代模型，它能够并行地读取与代理相同的上下文、模式和提议操作，并通过读出机制评估工具调用的正确性，从而提高错误检测和代码任务执行的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理可能因工具调用、查询和代码错误，在错误曝光前已经执行了相应的操作；并且前端模型因高度重复而难以识别错误。现有的方法无法有效检测这些错误。因此，研究者提出了一个低成本的开源替代模型来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 通过并行运行一个低成本的开源替代模型，该模型读取相同上下文并使用多种读出机制（如教师强迫、请求-PMI、判别性判决和工具选择竞争）独立评估每个模型的工具调用。根据生成的判别性和整体性判决，论文提出了一套决策方案来确定哪个模型的判断更可信。

Result: 该方法在困难的编码任务上达到了AUROC 0.825，远超过代理的自报信心（0.598），同时也优于其他方法，展示了其在提高错误检测和代码执行中的有效性。与自我一致性相比，该方法在几乎确定模型上获得了+0.14到+0.19的提升，且成本仅为原模型的1/K。

Conclusion: 该研究得出结论，通过并行运行一个低成本的开源替代模型来评估工具调用的正确性，对于提升代码任务执行的准确性是有效的。该方法提供了两种部署模式：实时闸门和信心反馈，以进一步提升自动化代理的操作准确性。

Abstract: A deployed LLM agent emits tool calls, queries, and code that can be silently wrong -- by the time the error surfaces, the action has run. Frontier chat APIs hide the model's token probabilities; the agent's stated confidence barely beats chance on the mistakes that matter; and resampling does not help, since frontier models are highly repetitive, reproducing the same call across samples.
  We recover the missing signal from a low-cost open-weight surrogate run in parallel. It reads the same context, schema, and proposed action as the agent, then scores the call from its own log-probabilities through a family of complementary readouts: teacher forcing and request-PMI weigh the likelihood of each argument value, a discriminative verdict judges the call as a whole, and tool-choice competition tests the function against its siblings. One principle says which to trust: a generative likelihood localizes wrong argument values, while the verdict catches holistically wrong calls. When the error type is unknown, an ensemble is the low-regret default. The readout is training-free, needs no access to the agent's internals, and costs one prefill pass alongside the tool call.
  On difficult coding tasks it reaches AUROC 0.825 where the actor's stated confidence is near chance (0.598), and the generative readouts beat it by +0.07 to +0.28 across three further actors. Against self-consistency it gains +0.14 to +0.19 on near-deterministic actors, at 1/K the cost. The signal drives two deployment modes: a real-time gate escalating the least-trustworthy calls for review (+0.05 to +0.30 accepted-action accuracy at 50% coverage), and confidence feedback, returning the tool result with the score so the agent adapts its next step -- lifting task success on live-execution benchmarks (+0.119 and +0.137, p <= 1e-4) and beating a random-value control where step errors are silent (+0.078, p = 0.003).

</details>


### [125] [SGAnalog: An End-to-End Circuit Benchmark from Open-Source Silicon Tapeouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03934)
*Yueting Li,Weihang Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该基准提供了273个独特电路的设计，用于评估电路转换和器件尺寸调整能力，发现模型在不同任务上的表现差异，指出标签可能有助于连通性识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模拟集成电路设计基准难以验证模型是否学习到可转移的电路技能而不仅仅是记忆特定示例，以及其输出是否在设定的工艺和测试条件下有效。这篇论文提出了一种新基准，利用小型封装设计制造平台收集的人工设计开源电路，以此解决上述问题。

Method: 该基准收集了273个高斯独的顶层设计，每个设计均在记录提交时的修订版本中获取，并在固定容器化环境中处理。基准评估了电路拓扑转换和器件尺寸调整的能力。此外，通过引入标签数据帮助模型识别连通性。

Result: 在七种模型上，针对固定66个电路转换任务，其中最强模型达56.1%的精确图同构率，部分模型在小到中等规模任务间表现差异显著。标签数据对于连通性识别有辅助作用。对于17项尺寸调整任务，最佳模型达到了91.2/100的评分，而最新型的Claude模型拒绝了一部分调整请求。

Conclusion: 该基准展示了在不同任务上模型的各种表现，并表明标签信息可能在某些方面对模型有积极影响。它还揭示了模型在视觉识别和设计能力方面的差异性局限，进一步指出未来研究可能需要基于不同策略进行改进。

Abstract: Existing analog integrated circuit design benchmarks make two questions hard to answer: whether a model has learned transferable circuit skills rather than recalled familiar examples, and whether its output works under defined process and test conditions. We introduce a benchmark built from human-designed, open-source circuits associated with Tiny Tapeout manufacturing shuttles. The collection contains 273 topologically distinct top-level designs. Every source is retrieved at the revision recorded for its shuttle submission and processed in a fixed containerized environment. The pipeline exports each eligible schematic image and its SPICE netlist from the same source file, giving transcription an exact structural reference. Commit dates support model-specific training-cutoff analysis, while author testbenches provide the simulation context for sizing. The benchmark evaluates schematic-to-netlist transcription and device sizing. Across seven models on a fixed set of 66 transcription tasks, the strongest model reaches 56.1% exact graph isomorphism, and six of seven models drop sharply from the small to the medium tier. For one frontier model, removing author-chosen labels reduces exact matches while preserving aggregate structural F1, suggesting that labels can aid connectivity tracing. On the 17 sizing tasks, the leading model converges on all 17 proposals and reaches 91.2 out of 100 against the human reference, while the two newest Claude models refuse 4 and 11 of the same prompts they transcribe without objection; a proposal without sizes scores zero. The two tasks produce different model rankings, exposing distinct visual and design capabilities and, in one family, a policy rather than capability limit.

</details>


### [126] [Request Order Matters: Cache-History Sensitivity in Selective KV-Cache Reuse for Rolling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05833)
*Tiffany Gu,Annie Guan,Manshu Huang,Nitin Rao,Siddhant Shah,Margaret Capetz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了长运行代理不断更新文档窗口对LLM缓存机制的影响，提出了一种基于文档对齐的选择性重计算方法，该方法能显著减少答案的不一致性，并提高了对完整预填充的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长运行代理在调用LLM时会保留大部分文档窗口，不断更新文档，这导致了缓存机制失效，特别是前缀缓存。研究旨在探索有效的缓存重利用策略，以减少计算负担并提高响应的一致性。

Method: 该研究设计了一种基于文档对齐的选择性重计算方法，该方法考虑了请求顺序的影响，通过减少不必要的重计算减少资源消耗，同时保留了缓存的效率。

Result: 这种方法在与CacheBlend的token top-$k$策略匹配的5%计算预算下，与之前的缓存策略相比，显著减少了答案的一致性变化，提高了对完整预填充的忠实度，并实现了更高的计算效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于文档的重计算是提高长运行代理请求处理能力的有效方法，尤其是在计算资源有限的情况下，具有潜在的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Long-running agents repeatedly call an LLM while retaining most of their document window, evicting old documents, and appending new ones. These rolling updates break exact prefix caching and motivate non-prefix KV-cache reuse with selective recomputation. We show that persistent KV-cache reuse with selective recomputation can be history-dependent: in our rolling-agent workload, an unchanged prompt can produce different answers depending on the requests processed before it. At a matched 5% recomputation budget, document-aligned recomputation reduces answer variation across request orders from 69.0% with CacheBlend's token top-$k$ policy to 26.1%. When each prompt is evaluated after a different sequence of preceding requests, document-aligned recomputation improves fidelity to full prefill by 34.5-52.5 percentage points over token top-$k$, while both policies achieve approximately 5.7$\times$ median TTFT speedup. Our ablation study shows that, in our rolling-agent workload, contiguity is the main factor associated with robust selective recomputation.

</details>


### [127] [MLLMs Fail to Refuse when Using Tools Agentically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03938)
*Rikiya Takehi,Ryo Hachiuma,Shaona Ghosh,Dan Zhao,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Yusuke Hirota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了基于工具使用的人工智能模型在处理安全问题时的能力下降，尤其是在使用视觉工具时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理型多模态大规模语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉推理方面取得了显著进步，但在使用工具时的安全性却成为了新的挑战。

Method: 通过对多个流行的评估基准数据进行对比实验，并对10万多个模型响应详细分析来研究模型在工具使用与非工具使用情景下的安全性差异。

Result: 研究发现，所有测试中的领先开放和闭合权重的MLLMs在使用工具时的安全性显著下降，相对抵抗有害请求失败率最高可达68.7%。

Conclusion: 两种潜在原因导致了这种安全下降现象，并提出了相应的研究方向。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have recently pushed the frontier of visual reasoning by calling tools such as zooming and tagging. Despite the recent strong success of agentic MLLMs, this work uncovers a critical safety failure in the tool-use paradigm: agentic tool-using MLLMs become less capable of refusing harmful requests. Our experiments confirm that, across three popular safety benchmarks, all the top open- and closed-weight MLLMs we test exhibit significantly lower safety in tool-using settings than in non-tool settings, with a relative refusal failure rate increase of up to 68.7%. Based on analysis of 100,000+ responses, including extended experiments, we also propose two possible reasons for this safety degradation.

</details>


### [128] [Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Model Decision-Making for Autonomous Driving Guided by Chinese Philosophical Wisdom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03948)
*Xiaojun Bi,Xiaoyuan Ma,Yiwen Sun,Tianren Huang,Chaoran Liu,Bokai Huang,Hao Yang,Baichuan Mo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CPW-Drive 是一种结合了中国哲学指导的自主驾驶决策框架，通过物理感知的空间相似性检索提高多车道高速公路驾驶任务的成功率和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主驾驶决策系统缺乏对安全、效率和社交规范的平衡，尤其是在基于数值优化、序列预测和大型语言模型的方法中。CPW-Drive 引入了中国哲学的价值引导，旨在提升决策系统的综合性能。

Method: CPW-Drive 使用了闭合回路检索增强生成（RAG）框架。它通过筛选和验证来自经典文献中的关键词，结合具体场景将价值原则转化为可检索和可重用的指导方针。还引入了物理感知的空间相似性检索（PSSR），通过比较车辆布局和速度状态来获取相关历史案例。

Result: 在高速公路环境下的多车道驾驶任务中，CPW-Drive 达到了 93.0%、86.0% 和 72.0% 的成功率，在三种交通配置中分别超越了最强基线 8.0、22.5 和 25.0 个百分点。此外，在所有配置下，CPW-Drive 的无碰撞步数最多，车道变换频率最低。

Conclusion: CPW-Drive 的结果表明结构化价值指导可以提高模拟闭环安全性和稳定性，尽管会引入效率和延迟的权衡。

Abstract: Autonomous driving decision systems must balance safety, efficiency, and social norms in complex traffic interactions. Philosophical and ethical considerations have received limited attention in existing autonomous driving decision-making approaches based on numerical optimization, sequence prediction, and large language models (LLMs). We propose Chinese Philosophical Wisdom-Guided Driving (CPW-Drive), a closed-loop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that incorporates value guidance derived from Chinese philosophy into autonomous driving decision-making. Using Chinese Confucian thought as its knowledge source, CPW-Drive consolidates LLM-extracted keywords from relevant classical texts into driving-relevant value principles through manual screening and validation. It then contextualizes these principles through scenario-specific cases to form retrievable and reusable value guidance. We further propose Physics-aware Spatial Similarity Retrieval (PSSR), which compares vehicle layouts and velocity-extrapolated states to retrieve physically relevant historical cases. On Highway-env's multilane highway-driving task, CPW-Drive achieves success rates of 93.0%, 86.0%, and 72.0% across three traffic configurations. These results outperform the strongest baseline by 8.0, 22.5, and 25.0 percentage points, respectively. Across all configurations, CPW-Drive achieves the highest collision-free step count and maintains a low lane-change frequency. The results suggest that structured value guidance can improve simulated closed-loop safety and stability while introducing efficiency and latency trade-offs.

</details>


### [129] [LatentQuant: Preserving the Policy-Facing Latent Contract under NVFP4 VAE Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03959)
*Ziye Deng,Lufang Chen,Shuyu Feng,Zhenwei Duan,Zicong Ye,Yu Sun,Xiaofan Li,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为LatentQuant的双阶段NVFP4量化框架，它首先将量化编码器与高精度副本对齐，然后冻结编码器以适应解码器，从而在保持控制接近基线和高质量重建的同时，实现显著的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs依赖预训练的视频VAE作为编码器，直接用于下游动作策略的条件，因此量化处理需要保持重建保真度和策略所需的主要特征，避免量化引入的误差影响模型性能。

Method: LatentQuant首先对量化编码器进行精细对齐以确保与高精度对照模型一致，然后在维持这一一致性的基础上冻结编码器以优化解码器参数。

Result: 在Wan2.1和Wan2.2数据集上，LatentQuant模型在LIBERO任务上实现了95.75%的成功率，在RoboTwin上则为68.8%，且实现了近乎实时的视频VAE处理速度，相较于BF16 cuDNN在NVIDIA B300 GPU上获得了1.17x至1.26x的端到端加速。

Conclusion: LatentQuant双阶段量化框架显著提高了WAM模型在保持重建质量的同时控制性能和计算效率，为实际应用提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent world action models (WAMs) reuse pretrained video VAEs whose encoder latents directly condition downstream action policies. Quantization must therefore preserve not only reconstruction fidelity but also the policy-facing latent contract expected by the frozen policy. Direct NVFP4 leaves W4A4 quantization error uncompensated, whereas joint quantization-aware training (QAT) can recover reconstruction by moving this representation. On Wan2.1, joint QAT nearly matches FP32 VBench-7 (0.7403 versus 0.7409), yet LIBERO success collapses from 95.5% to 10.5%. Controlled decoder-only experiments show that activation quantize-dequantize operations alter the reconstruction signal and decoder Jacobian, redirecting the gradient returned to the encoder and inducing persistent latent drift. Based on this mechanism, we introduce LatentQuant, a two-stage NVFP4 QAT framework that first aligns the quantized encoder with its high-precision counterpart, then freezes it while adapting the decoder. Across Wan2.1 and Wan2.2, LatentQuant preserves near-baseline control and high reconstruction quality, achieving 95.75% success on LIBERO and 68.8% on RoboTwin. On NVIDIA B300 GPUs, NVFP4 execution achieves 1.17x-1.26x end-to-end VAE speedups over BF16 cuDNN.

</details>


### [130] [ROAR: Unifying Runs across Heterogeneous AI-Driven Research Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03966)
*Leo Y. Lin,Vishakha Ramani,Z. Berkay Celik,Paul Castro,Marquita Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章提出了ROAR，一种统一和分析来自不同AI驱动研究系统（ADRS）的异构输出的方法。通过建立标准化的关系模式和解析层，ROAR能够整合和分析来自多个ADRS运行的数据，并揭示问题特异性搜索行为的结构，提供配置ADRS的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADRS运行昂贵且数据宝贵，现有数据分割和格式不统一的问题导致难以进行全面分析。因此，需要一种方法来系统地统一并分析来自不同ADRS的异构输出。

Method: ROAR通过建立一个关系模式和解析层，来标准化异构ADRS输出数据，记录数据源和时间结构，同时允许添加新系统而无需修改模式。从超过900个ADRS运行构建一个语料库，展示聚合数据如何揭示难以观察的属性。

Result: 研究表明，具有相同配置的运行可能达到不同的分数；大多数组运行在早期获得大部分收益，不同策略将先前解纳入搜索过程的有效性随问题而异。通过使用ROAR配置ADRS运行证明了聚合语料库不仅具有分析价值，也实用。

Conclusion: ROAR是首个设计用于统一ADRS运行的基础设施，表明聚合ADRS数据能够暴露从单一系统、团队或基准无法检测的问题依赖性结构搜索行为，从而为跨系统的配置ADRS提供了新的洞察力。

Abstract: Each run of an AI-driven research system (ADRS) is an expensive search over a vast solution space, and dependable evaluation requires many runs, making run data both costly to produce and valuable to retain for large-scale analysis. Yet this data remains fragmented: teams operate in isolation, ADRS frameworks emit results in different formats, and no shared infrastructure exists to aggregate or compare runs across problems and systems. We present ROAR, a solution for systematically unifying and analyzing heterogeneous ADRS outputs. ROAR addresses two challenges: reconciling heterogeneous ADRS outputs and enabling analytics across runs with different objectives and scoring functions. We achieve this through a relational schema and parsing layer that normalize heterogeneous ADRS outputs while preserving data lineage and temporal structure, and accommodating new systems without requiring schema modifications. From building a corpus of more than 900 runs from multiple ADRS, we show how pooled data can reveal properties of problem landscapes that are difficult to observe. Consistent with prior work, runs with identical configurations may converge to different scores. We find that many runs realize most gains early, and that the effectiveness of different strategies for incorporating prior solutions into the search process varies across problems. We further show that the pooled corpus is actionable and not merely analytical by using ROAR to configure ADRS runs. Together, these results illustrate how pooled ADRS data can expose problem-dependent structure in search behavior that is difficult to detect from any single system, team, or benchmark. Such cross-cutting insights are difficult to obtain while runs remain siloed; ROAR is the first infrastructure designed to unify them.

</details>


### [131] [Teaching Agents to Code Reliably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03984)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Myeongsoo Kim,Kangrui Ruan,Shweta Garg,Varun Kumar,Murali Krishna Ramanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种自主编码代理，通过阅读代码、执行命令、编辑文件和提交修复来解决仓库问题。通过利用执行反馈指导搜索，并使用每个修复与其回滚版本的对比评分，提高了问题解决率和验证器的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决软件仓库中的问题，当前方法依赖于大规模计算或附属构建，而这种研究旨在使这些行为可被训练而非只是规模的副产品，以使政策能够携带这些行为。

Method: 研究通过执行反馈指导搜索，并分别使用每个修复与其回滚版本进行对比评分来解决问题。还采用了加权监督微调和强化学习目标来训练验证器。

Result: 通过执行反馈和政策评分，解决了52.8%的SWE-bench问题，比八样本基线减少了资源消耗的48.1%。对于保留的270个问题，验证器的准确性也得到了提升，pass@1从31.9%提升到43.0%，pass@8从46.7%提升到60.7%，假阳性率降低了超过一半。

Conclusion: 这种自主编码代理能够提高问题解决率和验证器的准确性，并且在规模更大的模型中得到了验证，展示出了可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents solve repository issues by reading code, running commands, editing files, and submitting patches. Extra inference-time compute yields gains only when it produces a useful repair and supplies reliable evidence for choosing one. Three behaviors decide both, and we argue they are teachable rather than byproducts of scale, so a policy can carry them instead of a scaffold. Location diversity remains narrow, since attempts return to the same site and extra samples add no coverage. Edit diversity is left unexploited, since methodologies that differ resolve complementary issues no single run reaches. Verification misleads, since a test the agent writes for its own patch accepts many incorrect ones. Directing search by execution feedback and scoring each patch against its own reverted tree resolves 52.8% of SWE-bench Verified using 48.1% of the agent-steps an eight-sample baseline spends. Training moves these behaviors into the policy. On the 270 issues held out from SFT and RL training, weighted supervised fine-tuning raises pass@1 from 31.9% to 35.2% and pass@8 from 46.7% to 51.1%. A reinforcement objective then trains the verifier against gold-labeled repairs and incorrect variants, crediting the assertions that detect them. It raises pass@1 to 43.0% and pass@8 to 60.7%, lifts verifier precision from 26.8% to 41.7%, and more than halves false acceptance. Resolution improves on two of three out-of-distribution suites and verifier precision on all three, and the gains hold at 7B, 14B, and 30B against published coder baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Behavioral History Outperforms Descriptions of the Person for LLM Synthetic Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03998)
*Khashayar Pourtaheri,Ahmad Zareei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了不同条件下LLM合成人物（代表调查受访者）预测个体后续选择的有效性。通过两种轮次的面板调查，研究发现尽管LLM在人群中总体偏差数量与人类相似，但在个体层面，仅基于描述的合成人物表现较差，而加入行为历史条件显著提升了预测能力。同时，LLM合成人物表现出更强的教育和收入相关差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估LLM作为调查受访者合成代表的有效性，特别是它们如何捕捉个人特征和未来的决策行为。

Method: 研究使用两轮面板调查845名美国成年人，涵盖了14种行为偏差测验，通过逐步增加受访者的个人信息（从无个人信息到完整的个人行为历史）来测试合成人物的预测能力。

Result: 研究结果表明，LLM合成人物在描述个人特征时表现不佳，但加入行为历史信息可以显著提高预测准确性。在个体层面，包含行为历史的条件表现出最高的预测效能，而非基于描述的条件则几乎没有提供有用信息。此外，LLM合成人物还表现出更显著的教育和收入相关偏差。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，对于LLM合成人物，受访者的过去答案比对其描述更能有效预测个体的行为。这一发现强调了在使用合成代表时考虑行为历史的重要性，这可能有助于提高调查结果的准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as synthetic personas representing survey respondents. Their validity as substitutes for particular respondents depends on whether they reproduce individuals' decisions. We examine what information helps synthetic respondents predict each individual's later choices, using five conditions that add progressively richer information: no personal information, demographics, personality traits, cognitive scores, and finally the respondent's earlier survey choices as behavioral history. We use a two-wave panel of 845 US adults who completed measures of 14 behavioral biases (spanning risk, time preferences, overconfidence, and reasoning), so each respondent's earlier answers provide a human test-retest benchmark; in the behavioral-history condition, all items that score the target bias are withheld. At the population level, the average number of biases per respondent in every condition is close to the human average (7.1-8.1 biases, against 7.1 for humans). This aggregate similarity masks differences in variance: persona descriptions recover only 53-67% of human between-person variation, whereas adding behavioral history restores it to approximately the human level. At the individual level, description-based personas achieve only 7-12% of the informedness observed in human test-retest responses, while adding behavioral history raises this to 28%. The condition including behavioral history has the highest estimated informedness in all 17 demographic groups, whereas description-based conditions provide little or no information for some groups. Synthetic responses also exhibit stronger education- and income-related differences than human responses. For LLM synthetic personas, a respondent's past answers add more to individual-level prediction than a description of who they are.

</details>


### [133] [SkillScriptBench: Benchmarking Self-Evolution of Executable Agent Skill Packages Beyond Markdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04008)
*Yuxuan Liu,Haoran Li,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Hongyu Luo,Wenbin Hu,Huihao Jing,Kawai Chung,Junle Chen,Changxuan Fan,Qing Zong,Lingyun Xie,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SkillScriptBench基准，这是一个包含350个任务的评估框架，专门针对技能文档修复、脚本修复和行为保持能力。通过引入AST-Guided技能修订方法，根据抽象语法树和调用关系链接维护需求，限制修改脚本位置，更新文档以匹配修订后的脚本，从而提高了修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未系统地区分文档修复、脚本修复和行为保持，现有的方法在这三个方面均有不足，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 首先，通过收集GitHub上35,000多个技能项目的根目录，挑选出100个包，构建了150个修复任务。其中每个任务包含带注释的脚本故障包，并匹配维护请求和可执行的行为检查。此外，还设立了200个互补的受控实验性任务，评估标准化。为了解决现有方法的缺陷，作者提出了一种基于抽象语法树的技能修订方法，这种方法能将维护需求与相关代码位置进行关联，并限制脚本修改，更新文档以匹配修订脚本。

Result: 在四个LLM模型上，编辑文档和脚本的方法能够修复脚本故障，但在文档修复和保持上的表现不佳。引入的AST-Guided技能修订方法能够大幅提高修复成功率，尤其是在有脚本故障的包中，原始包和CoEvoSkills的修复成功率分别提高了21.9%和27.7%。此外，在所有三种运行中的任务解决比例的绝对增益分别达到了20.8%和31.5%，显示出更一致的修复成功率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建SkillScriptBench基准和提出AST-Guided技能修订方法，显著改善了对技能修复的综合评估，并展示了如何通过目标语言的内部结构来提高修复结果的一致性。

Abstract: Executable Agent Skills combine natural-language instructions and scripts into reusable packages for LLM agents, and revising them requires fixing errors without breaking correct behavior. Existing benchmarks do not systematically distinguish documentation repair, script repair, and preservation when evaluating skill self-evolution. We introduce SkillScriptBench, a 350-task benchmark designed to evaluate these capabilities separately. From a survey of over 35,000 GitHub-hosted Skill roots, we select 100 packages and construct 150 repair tasks. Each task pairs a package containing injected script faults with a maintenance request and executable checks of the required behavior. A complementary controlled track contains 200 tasks from 50 packages, each evaluated under the same maintenance request in four states: clean, documentation faults, script faults, and faults in both. Across four LLMs, methods that edit both documentation and scripts can repair script faults but do not consistently outperform Markdown-only revision on documentation repair or preservation. We therefore introduce AST-Guided Skill Revision, which uses abstract syntax trees and calling relationships to link maintenance requirements to relevant code locations. It restricts script edits to these locations and updates the documentation to match the revised scripts. Averaged across models, this revision stage yields absolute gains in repair success of 21.9% for Raw Package and 27.7% for CoEvoSkills on faulty packages. Absolute gains in the proportion of tasks solved in all three runs reach 20.8% and 31.5%, respectively, indicating more consistent repair success across repeated runs.

</details>


### [134] [Exploration-Preserving Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04011)
*Hangzhan jin,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExPPO 是一种优势重分配规则，通过对响应的意外程度和指令通过率进行长度归一化处理，确保验证者极性的保存以及每个指令组的绝对序列优势质量的大致保持。实验显示，ExPPO 提高了领域内和领域外的推理覆盖率、总体响应准确率，并在大量采样预算下表现出强大覆盖能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习中，学习信号的分配方式对模型解的可访问性有重要影响。ExPPO 方法通过引入反应相对值的意外程度和指令通过率进行归一化处理的优势重分配规则，确保了验证者极性的保持和每个指令组的绝对序列优势质量的大致保持，从而改善了解释的质量，并提高了模型的多样性和准确性。

Method: ExPPO 通过响应相对值的意外程度和长度归一化的指令通过率来重新分配信用，同时结合限制型优势重分配和共享规范化，以保持验证者的极性和大致维护每个指令组的序列优势。这种方法通过对采样模式的更新来推导出局部条件，从而在提高熵和正确模式发现方面取得进展。

Result: ExPPO 实验证明了在领域内和领域外推理覆盖范围的提升，总体响应准确率的提升，并且在较大的采样预算下展示了强大的覆盖能力。此外，控制下的多答案评估进一步表明正确模式产出的增加和被验证正确响应多样性方面的改进。

Conclusion: ExPPO 方法通过重新分配优势信号来改进了模型的解释质量，通过结合响应的意外程度、指令通过率和采样模式更新等策略，为强化学习中的有效学习提供了一种机制。未来的研究可以进一步探讨如何更好地将 ExPPO 应用于更复杂的场景。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning, while the allocation of learning signal shapes which solutions remain accessible under repeated sampling. Group-relative objectives assign equal advantages to equally rewarded responses, making aggregate credit proportional to sampled mode frequency. We introduce Exploration-Preserving Policy Optimization (ExPPO), a lightweight advantage-shaping rule that redistributes credit using prompt-relative, length-normalized response surprisal and prompt pass rate. ExPPO combines bounded shaping with shared normalization to preserve verifier polarity and approximately maintain each prompt group's total absolute sequence-advantage mass. Our analysis characterizes response-level credit allocation alongside sampled mode updates, deriving local conditions for gains in entropy and correct-mode discovery. Experiments show improved in-domain and out-of-domain reasoning coverage, higher aggregate response accuracy, and strong coverage at large sampling budgets. A controlled multi-answer evaluation further demonstrates increased correct-mode yield and gains in diversity among verified-correct responses. Code is available at https://github.com/jinhangzhan/ExPPO

</details>


### [135] [Beyond the Parameter Monolith: Reconstructive Memories, Executable Skills, and Residual Assembly for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04012)
*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种类似于有限元方法的语言模型组织FEM-ASM，在该模型中，独立构建的文档状态和确定性的执行技能向共享的语言模型状态提交类型化的建议，通过显式的残差算子解决共同接口节点绑定的建议。尽管原型在因果语言建模方面表现不俗，但在整体执行能力上未能超越现有模型。模型的存储、执行和神经协调分离有助于分离计算、持久存储和精确执行，但发现了一些未解决的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现语言模型更高效的计算与处理方式，通过对现有单一参数系统进行拆分，分离出各自独立的组件，并通过共享语言模型状态进行协调。

Method: 提出了FEM-ASM模型组织，该模型借鉴了有限元方法，采用独立的文档状态和执行技能提交建议给共享状态，使用显式的残差算子来整合这些建议。还设计了一个无注意力的多网格原型来试验因果语言建模，并对存储器和可执行的算术进行了详细实验。

Result: 注意力无关的多网格原型能够进行因果语言建模，但整体执行能力不如现有模型；存储器版本包含52,809个重构记忆元素，在近1.7亿浮点值预算下；支持感知的词表索引为这些元素提供了基于来源控制的查询处理方式；对于可执行的算术，位置结果观察比全局结果向量提高了神经渲染效果，输出替换也能改变模型的答案偏好；边界附着示范测试了提供选择性证据的效果。这些结果表明存储、执行和神经协调分离是有效的，但也显示出在问题检索、无限制的答案生成和端到端效率方面的局限。

Conclusion: 尽管FEM-ASM模型组织取得了初步成果，但在整体执行能力、完整重建和端到端效率方面仍存在不足。未来的研究需要进一步解决这些问题，提高模型的整体能力。

Abstract: Language-model systems can separate contextual computation, persistent storage, and exact execution instead of updating all capabilities through one shared parameter system. We investigate FEM-ASM, a finite-element-method-inspired organization in which independently constructed document states and deterministic executable skills contribute typed proposals to a shared language-model state. An explicit residual operator reconciles proposals attached to common interface nodes. We evaluate this organization through controlled experiments and negative results rather than claiming a physical finite-element formulation of language. An attention-free Multi-Mesh prototype learns causal language modeling but does not establish competitive general capability. A versioned store contains 52,809 reconstructive memory elements near a 1.7-billion-floating-value budget; reconstruction is incomplete, with approximately 75\% token accuracy. Support-aware lexical indices make these elements addressable under provenance-controlled query construction. For executable arithmetic, positional result observations substantially improve neural rendering relative to a repeated global result vector, and output substitutions change the model's preferred answer. A bounded attachment demonstration further measures the effect of making selected evidence available, without establishing the utility of loading an entire multi-billion-value store. The results support a separation of storage, execution, and neural coordination, while identifying unresolved limitations in question-only retrieval, unrestricted answer generation, and end-to-end efficiency.

</details>


### [136] [A Quantitative Analysis of Graph Representation Strategies for Cyber Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04019)
*Ali Melih Kanca,Ilker Turker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究量化分析了2019年至2026年间发表的37篇基于图的网络安全攻击检测论文，发现自动化表示学习策略被广泛采用，而手工构造的表示策略较少使用，并且某些网络安全应用领域更倾向于使用特定的表示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过定量分析现有文献，揭示网络安全攻击检测中使用的图表示策略的多样性和分布情况，以指导未来的研究方向。

Method: 研究收集了2019年至2026年间共37篇相关论文的数据，按照发表年份、应用领域、图表示类型、特征抽取策略、学习范式、算法和数据集进行编码。然后通过频次分析、交叉表和统计关联性测试来分析数据。

Result: 研究结果表明，自动化表示学习策略在所有论文中占73.0%，手工构造的表示策略占27.0%，并且网络安全应用领域与表示学习策略之间存在统计学上的显著关联。

Conclusion: 自动化表示学习策略和手工构造表示策略在网络安全攻击检测中的应用比例不同，且不同应用领域存在不同的表示策略偏好。

Abstract: Graph based cyber attack detection studies employ various graph construction and representation strategies across different cybersecurity application domains. This diversity motivates a quantitative examination of how representation strategies are distributed across these application domains. This study presents a quantitative analysis of 37 original studies published between 2019 and 2026. Each study was coded according to publication year, application domain, graph representation type, feature extraction strategy, learning paradigm, algorithm, and dataset. Frequency analysis, cross tabulation, and statistical association tests were applied. Automated representation learning was the most frequently employed strategy, accounting for 73.0% of the studies, while handcrafted representation strategies accounted for 27.0%. The Fisher Freeman Halton exact test revealed a statistically significant association between application domain and representation strategy (exact p = 0.008; Cramer's V = 0.540). The findings indicate that automated representation learning predominates within the analyzed corpus and that the distribution of representation strategies differs across cybersecurity application domains.

</details>


### [137] [The Reported Engagement with AI Level (REAL) Rating: A Framework for Disclosing Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04021)
*Imène Goumiri,Mayleen Cortez-Rodriguez,Eric Bell,Amanda Muyskens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种名为REAL评级框架，通过六级分层系统详细披露了AI在其生产和消费过程中的参与程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的产权标准和披露方法经常依赖二元分类，但这种分类无法准确反映AI在内容创作中的多样性和潜在风险。因此，提出REAL评级框架来细化AI参与程度的披露。

Method: 该论文详细介绍了REAL评级系统的结构化方法，并探讨了其在文本、图像、音频、视频和软件产品中的应用。

Result: 提出了一个六级分层系统，具体应用于各种数字媒体产品中，旨在提高用户对其来源和透明度的理解。

Conclusion: REAL评级框架提供了一种新的方法，通过提供更多的透明度和详细信息，促进了公众对AI生成内容的理解。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has become increasingly incorporated into digital media and more generally into production workflows with which the public frequently interacts. Current provenance standards and disclosure methods frequently rely on binary categorizations, differentiating only between entirely human-authored and AI-generated content, or depend on technical watermarking that lacks user-facing clarity. However, there are very different risks and outcomes depending on the different uses of AI in the creation and consumption of end products. Therefore, we propose the Reported Engagement with AI Level (REAL) Rating framework which addresses these concerns by introducing a six-tier scale (Levels 0-5) that discloses the extent of AI involvement in both product creation and user experience. This paper details the structural methodology of the REAL Rating system and the specifics of its application across text, image, audio, video, and software products.

</details>


### [138] [Agent Policy-Value Audit: Separating Transition Composition from Event Selection in Financial LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04040)
*Mingyang,Chen,Yida,Xu,Huiwen,Chen,Yiming Lu,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了代理政策-价值审计方法，通过随机重新分配观察到的每一类行动变化的次数，来区分部署价值与事件选择价值，从而更准确地评估金融LLM代理的真实表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将部署变化与事件选择混淆，导致误将被动暴露归因于选择能力。研究旨在通过新的审计方法提高评估准确性。

Method: 研究提出了一种名为代理政策-价值审计的方法，固定了每种行动变化的观察次数，然后随机重新分配这些变化到不同的事件上，并计算重新分配后的平均收益作为基准（组成基准），再用代理的实际部署价值与基准进行比较。

Result: 在半合成基准测试中，零中心配对检验错误归因了11.6% 的无技能复制中的被动暴露到选择能力中，而时间匹配审计将这一比率降低到5.3%。在回顾44家美国消费品公司的723次盈余事件后，代理的收益被分解为25.8bps 的组成基准和-10.6bps 的选择价值。

Conclusion: 总之，代理政策-价值审计方法能够更准确地分离出部署价值和事件选择价值，对于评价金融LLM代理的实际能力非常关键，不应将部署价值与事件选择价值混为一谈。

Abstract: Financial LLM agents are often evaluated by comparing their end-to-end returns with those of a baseline and testing the paired difference against zero. This measures whether deploying the agent changes realized performance, but it does not isolate event-selection skill. An agent that frequently changes positions from flat to long can earn a positive paired return from an upward-drifting event pool even when it selects events at random. We propose the Agent Policy-Value Audit, which holds fixed the observed count of each ordered action-change type and randomly reassigns them across eligible events. The average payoff from these reassignments is the composition benchmark; the difference between observed deployment value and this benchmark is selection value. In semi-synthetic benchmarks based on real earnings-event returns, a zero-centered paired test falsely attributes passive exposure to selection skill in $11.6\%$ of no-skill replications, while the transition-matched audit reduces this rate to $5.3\%$. Applied retrospectively to 723 earnings events at 44 U.S. consumer-facing firms, the audit decomposes the agent's gross deployment value of $+15.2$ bps/event into a $+25.8$ composition benchmark and a $-10.6$ selection value. The agent does not detectably outperform matched random assignments. Financial-agent evaluations should report deployment value separately from event-selection value.

</details>


### [139] [The Cost of a Hop: Benchmarking NLIP and A2A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04053)
*Ranjan Sinha,Anindita Das,Ashika Anand Babu,Hari Palleti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了NLIP协议和A2A协议在不同硬件环境下的性能表现，发现NLIP在消息创建和连接阶段显著优于A2A，但在LLM推理占主导的端到端管道中，两者接近平价。通过启用连接缓存，A2A可以缩小与NLIP的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前存在多种代理间通信协议，但尚未有针对代理协议延时的具体研究。本文填补了这方面空白，通过实测对比NLIP和A2A协议在不同硬件环境下的性能。

Method: 通过在三个不同硬件环境中实测NLIP和A2A协议的延迟，将其分解为消息创建、连接和发送阶段，并使用连接缓存优化A2A协议的具体方法。

Result: NLIP在两个硬件环境下比A2A快8.4-9.6倍，另一个环境下快约4倍；连接阶段是NLIP领先的关键，而在LLM推理阶段二者接近平价。通过连接缓存优化A2A，其与NLIP的性能差距有所缩小，具体优化效果依赖于硬件性能。

Conclusion: 本文结果提供了在选择协议时的工作负载特征导向指南，表明在轻量级协调场景下应优先考虑NLIP协议。

Abstract: Autonomous agents built on Large Language Models (LLMs) need standardized protocols to interoperate across systems. Several now exist (A2A, MCP, ACP, ANP, NLIP), but the Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP) has not appeared in any controlled performance study, and no work has measured where an agent protocol's latency is spent. We compare NLIP and the Agent-to-Agent (A2A) protocol empirically, decomposing latency into message creation, connection, and send phases across three independent hardware environments. For lightweight coordination, NLIP is 8.4-9.6x faster than the baseline A2A SDK implementation on two environments and about 4x on a third; the direction of the advantage is consistent, its magnitude depends on the hardware. The advantage is stage-specific: for the end-to-end pipeline, where LLM inference dominates, the protocols are near parity. The difference comes almost entirely from connection setup. To test A2A at its best, we also ran A2A SDK with connection caching enabled; caching narrows its gap with NLIP by a hardware-dependent amount, from 2.75x on one machine to near-parity on faster hardware, where at scale a cache-optimized A2A-SDK matches NLIP. We report these as measured conditions without a single causal account of the residual send-phase cost. Against the more optimized Python-A2A, NLIP leads by about 4x on the same stage. We close with a protocol-selection guide keyed to workload characteristics.

</details>


### [140] [Discrete Diffusion for Large Graph Generation via Structural Candidate Restriction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04056)
*Yassin Mohamadi,Zeno Geradts,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于离散图扩散模型的大规模图生成方法，该方法通过限制训练到结构上有意义的节点对子集，降低了训练复杂性，并设计了一种度感知的吸收前向过程来保持图在生成过程中的信息性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型稀疏图的生成中，现有的非二次复杂度方法虽然在可扩展性和生成质量上取得了进展，但仍受到记忆化问题的限制。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出了一种新的离散图扩散模型，该模型通过结构驱动的方式减少训练复杂性，同时保持生成图的质量。

Method: 本文通过一种结构驱动的方法，限制训练到观察到的边和它们的三元非边，从而将训练复杂性降低到次二次。同时，设计了一种度感知的吸收前向过程，使用一种度感知的余弦噪声时间计划，以适应每个节点的度，保持生成过程中的图信息性。

Result: 实验表明，该模型在大型图生成中具有较高的结构一致性，并在与现有离散扩散基线的结构保真度方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法具有较低的训练复杂性，并在保持图结构信息的同时产生了高质量的大型图。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic graphs at scale is vital when the graphs of interest are large and real-world samples are limited or access-sensitive. Diffusion-based generators have recently driven much of the progress, offering high modeling capacity, but most such methods have quadratic computational complexity and are hence restricted to small-scale networks, currently up to 3k nodes. Existing non-quadratic methods remain limited by memorization issues and a trade-off between scalability and generation quality. Our goal is to generate large graphs whose structural statistics --- e.g., degree distribution, clustering, and path length --- faithfully reflect those of real-world sparse graphs, without resorting to memorizing the training data. We introduce a discrete graph diffusion model that restricts training to a structurally motivated subset of node pairs --- observed edges and their wedge non-edges --- reducing training complexity below quadratic in the number of nodes. To keep the noisy graph informative throughout both the forward and reverse trajectories, we design a three-class absorbing forward process governed by a \emph{degree-aware, floored} cosine noise schedule: unlike a structure-blind schedule that adds noise to every pair identically, ours adapts to each node's degree and never fully erases the graph's structure, keeping the noisy graph informative at every step. Experiments across diverse datasets show that our model consistently ranks among the top methods for structural fidelity against existing discrete diffusion baselines in large graph generation.

</details>


### [141] [Reinforcement Learning with Comparative Evidence for Social Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04072)
*Keane Ong,Yuriel Ryan,Sabri Boughorbel,Vladimir Necula,Jack Wei Lun Shi,Rui Mao,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Adriel Kuek,Nancy F. Chen,Erik Cambria,Gianmarco Mengaldo,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RLCE的方法，它通过无标签数据自主学习社会理解，无需从真实标注中构建奖励信号。研究在四个基准上的表现优于其他无标注奖励的方法，特别是在奖励变化和政策修正方面表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于目前的AI社会智能模型主要依赖于人类标注数据，范围和深度受限，本文旨在探索从非标数据中学习社会理解的方法，减少对人工标注数据的依赖。

Method: RLCE方法通过对比证据来构建证据测试，以验证不同回答的支持情况，并基于这些测试结果选择最可能的解释。随着策略产生新的回答，这些测试会更新以适应策略的发展。

Result: 在四个涵盖情感、语用、交际意图和偏好领域的基准测试中，相对于不使用真实标注奖励信号的其它七种方法，RLCE方法表现最优，相较于最强基线方法的改进甚至达到 +18.93 分。

Conclusion: 研究结果显示，RLCE方法在处理社交数据的预测时表现出色，尤其是在奖励分布和纠正错误策略方面优于基于评价标准的方法。

Abstract: Developing socially intelligent AI remains heavily dependent on human-annotated data, limiting the scale and breadth of social understanding models can acquire.
